  `patterns_for_unboxed_fields` with no function slot, with
  `classify_field_map`, and their `fold_destructuring` with
  `destructure_expanded_head` at the accessors of the patterns,
  `destructure_field`; the fields of an unboxed `my_closure` are
  parameters with no equation). Where the OCaml stops with a fatal error,
  a leaf has no variable and the unknown type. None of it is extracted,
  and the extracted definitions are unchanged.
  `rewrite_unboxed_result_type_sound`: the type that the result types of
  the code rebuilt with unboxing give each field of an unboxed variable
  of code, at any depth of unboxing, holds of the value of the variable
  of that field in the run of the unit rebuilt with unboxing (the mode
  of the fix, with no change of representation), given the value
  `v_mid` of the rebuild without unboxing at the variable, related to
  that of the first run by `rebuilt_value`, and the values of the
  fields, related to `v_mid` by `unboxed_values` (which is `fields_vals`
  of the simulation of the unboxing, `unboxed_values_fields_vals`). Two
  premises are new: `check_unboxed_uses`, of the fragment of the
  unboxing proofs (no use of an unboxed name is called or has a
  function slot read from it: otherwise `add_usages_through_function_slots`
  adds uses whose fields the allocation need not use, nor their
  metadata hold where the field is), and `block_fields_of_one_kind`, of
  the graph (an allocation holds a field of a block at one kind, as the
  traversal gives: a field unboxed in turn is read at the kind of the
  read). No fault found. Left: the closure types of a set whose
  representation changes, which the model forgets where
  `types_rewriter.ml` forgets them only for several local sets or a
  possible external source, and otherwise rewrites them into the new
  representation (`exactly_this_closure` at the new slots); unboxed
  closures; closure types that are not aliases; and unnamed closures.
- The rewriting of types, closure types that are not aliases, unnamed
  closures and changes of representation (`FlambdaReaperTypes.v`,
  `FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v` and its proofs). No fifth fault. The closure
  of another function slot of a set is rewritten under `Single_source`
  of its allocation, which may have no use and other sources, so that it
  is not rebuilt under that metadata as `rebuilt_under` has it; but it is
  rebuilt with its set, and that is all the closure case reads: the set
  recovered is the same, the value slots are those of the set, and the
  code id is erased where that closure is not called, as the rebuild
  deletes its code. `rewrite_unaliased_type_sound` and
  `rewrite_unaliased_symbol_type_sound`: the first two statements for the
  fragment whose closure types need not be aliases
  (`type_in_fragment_with true`, of `shaped_type_with`; the fragment of
  the other statements is `shaped_type_with false`, and they are
  unchanged). The proof relates a closure to its rebuild by its
  allocation, described by the metadata, and not by a node
  (`closure_under`, in the proofs), so that no premise on uses or
  sources is needed. One premise is new, on the runs: the static sets
  are kept whole (`static_sets_whole`), since the closure of a static set
  at another slot is a symbol that may have no use, of which
  `statics_rebuilt` says nothing. The rebuild keeps a static set whole
  (`static_const_is_used`) and a well-formed run binds it whole
  (`static_sets_bound`), but the `statics_rel` of the simulation lets the
  second run leave unbound a symbol of a static set that has no use, so
  that the premise is not proved of the runs, and the statements of
  result types and of the two runs are not extended. Unnamed closures
  (`Value_closure`, `Static_closure`) are not covered: only the older
  forms `Set_of_closures` and `Static_const_set_of_closures` make them,
  which no term of the compiler is converted to and `in_flow_fragment`
  excludes; `rel`, `statics_rel` and `flows` have no case for them; the
  traversal gives them no constructor at a function slot, so that
  `set_sites` and `sets_of_graph` cannot identify their set; and the
  rewriting reads a set by its function slots, where they are at
  `unnamed_function_slot`, which a named set may have too. The closure
  types of a set whose representation changes are transcribed
  (`rewrite_arbitrary_u`, `changed_rule`, `rewrite_changed`,
  `changed_leaves`: `exactly_this_closure` at the new function slot,
  every function slot at its new slot with its code id unless never
  called, the closure types under `Single_source` of each closure, the
  new value slots by `patterns_for_unboxed_fields` with the function
  slots; forgotten for several sets or a possible external source), not
  extracted; the extracted definitions are unchanged. `changed_example`:
  on `block_in_slot`, the closure type of `x60` becomes that of the
  closure at 82 with `= 7` at 81, where `rewrite_arbitrary` forgets it,
  and it holds of the closure of the run rebuilt with unboxing, related
  to the closure of the run rebuilt without it as `crel` relates them
  (`changed_closure_rebuilt`; `changed_example_crel` in the proofs), and
  through `rebuilt_under` to the closure of the unit. Left: the
  statement of changed representations for every closure (of the three
  runs, as for unboxed variables of code); `static_sets_whole` of the
  runs (a `statics_rel` that keeps static sets whole), and with it the
  end-to-end statement for closure types that are not aliases; unboxed
  closures.
- The rewriting of types, closure types that are not aliases at the exit,
  and local fields (`FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v` and its proofs,
  `FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.v`). `static_sets_whole` holds of the runs
  of the exit theorem. `rebuild_exit_relation_whole` is
  `rebuild_exit_relation` with `static_sets_whole` of the static
  environments of the two exits, and `rebuild_exit_relation_whole_holds`
  proves it under the premises of `rebuild_exit_relation_holds`.
  `statics_rel` is unchanged: the invariant added is one of the second
  run alone. The rebuild keeps the sets of a term well-formed
  (`rebuild_sets_well_formed`, in the proofs). It keeps the function
  slots of a set, and keeps or removes the bindings of a static set
  together, since `static_const_is_used` reads only the symbols of the
  set. It rebuilds each binding into at most one of the same code ids
  and symbols, which keeps `static_sets_code_disjoint`. So
  `FlambdaClosuresProofs.sets_inv` gives `static_sets_bound` of every
  state of the run of the rebuilt unit of a well-formed unit
  (`rebuilt_sets_bound`). With `static_sets_bound` of the first run
  (`well_formed_sets_bound`) and the closure case of `statics_rel` (a
  symbol with a use is bound in the second run, to a closure of the same
  set), `static_sets_whole` follows (`statics_rel_whole`). New
  statements: `rewrite_unaliased_result_type_sound` (result types, for
  the fragment `loose`), `exported_unaliased_types_sound` (the two runs,
  given `rebuild_exit_relation_whole`), `rebuild_exit_relation_whole_holds`
  and `exported_unaliased_types_sound_end_to_end` (no premise on the
  relation of the runs). Nothing found false. Left: the result types of
  unboxed variables of code for closure types that are not aliases
  (`rewrite_unboxed_result_type_sound` is of the fragment of aliases).

  Local fields: each statement of the types files that asks a least
  solution or the old premise has a `_local` version, in a section of its
  own at the end of `FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v`. These are
  `rewrite_type_sound_local`, `rewrite_symbol_type_sound_local`,
  `rewrite_unaliased_type_sound_local`,
  `rewrite_unaliased_symbol_type_sound_local`,
  `rewrite_result_type_sound_local`,
  `rewrite_unaliased_result_type_sound_local`,
  `rewrite_unboxed_result_type_sound_local`,
  `exported_types_sound_local`, `exported_unaliased_types_sound_local`,
  `rebuild_exits_local`, `rebuild_exit_relation_holds_local`,
  `rebuild_exit_relation_whole_holds_local`,
  `exported_types_sound_end_to_end_local` and
  `exported_unaliased_types_sound_end_to_end_local`. Those of a unit ask
  `check_local_reads_used` (or `least_solution_local`) in place of
  `(∀ f, is_local f = false) ∨ projection_free e = true` (and of
  `least_solution`). The five old ones (`rebuild_exits`,
  `rebuild_exit_relation_holds`, `rebuild_exit_relation_whole_holds` and
  the two end-to-end ones) are now corollaries, through
  `old_premise_local_reads_used` and `least_local_of_least`. Those of a
  graph and its tables ask `least_solution_local` in place of
  `least_solution`, and have no old premise. Neither version implies the
  other, as the least solution of the fixed rules may lose tuples of
  `usages` (`local_fix_drops_usages`). Both are proved of a least
  solution of either rules (`least_either`, in the proofs). The proofs
  read four facts of a least solution: it is closed, it has no stale
  tuple, `usages_carry_sources` and `has_source_sources`. Each is proved
  of `least_solution_local` by the same induction on the derivations of
  `derivable_local`. `FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.v` has
  `rebuild_exit_statics_local` (`slots_read` is `True`, and the check
  gives the rule of the fix), and `rebuild_exit_statics` is its
  corollary.
- The arm of `make_apply_wrapper` that the fix leaves stubbed (`Unbox _,
  (Keep _ | Delete) | Keep _, Unbox _`, "CR ncourant: restore this
  check"), in the mode of the fix (`FlambdaReaperWrapper{Spec,Proofs}.v`):
  it is reached only where the callee's result has no source, so the test
  `has_any_source` could be restored, but the arm cannot be an
  unconditional fatal error. For every direct call that
  `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed` (or `_tail`) rebuilds, in the unit and
  in the code it keeps, at every position, the decisions are the arm only
  where no node that the call reads off its callee with the field of
  known arity has any source, or a source whose result of that rank has a
  source (`callee_result_has_source`: `not_local_field_has_source` at that
  node; `arm_needs_no_source`, and `arm_needs_no_source_auto` with the
  flag `Auto`, which drops `check_apply_widgets`). This covers code that
  changes its calling convention, code that keeps it (which never returns
  to an unboxed parameter but where its result has no source) and code
  that is not known. A result that the callee unboxes has a source, so
  `Keep _, Unbox _` is never reached (`kept_result_never_unboxed`). Calls
  of unknown arity with one group of arguments: the same, at the field of
  unknown arity (`indirect_arm_needs_no_source`); with several groups,
  whose results go through a chain of witnesses, they are not lifted to
  the unit. The calls (`rebuilt_calls`) are collected as `rebuild_expr_u`
  binds the continuations, more of them where it drops a term. The
  argument (`result_flow`, for any call): where the result `r` flows into
  the parameter `x` (`alias x r`), a used `x` is dominated by any
  allocation to unbox that dominates `r`, as a use of `x` ends, by
  aliases, in a read with a used result or in any usage, which the two
  seeds of the fix and `cu0_any_usage` forbid; an `r` with a source is
  dominated by what dominates `x`; and an unboxed `x` takes no result
  with a source of code that cannot change (`cu0_result`), which is all
  code that a call of unknown arity, or of two code ids, reaches. Premises
  beyond a least solution and the checks of the fix (`check_dominated`,
  `check_to_unbox_fixed`): the fragment of the flow proof,
  `check_cc_closed`, `check_apply_widgets`, and checks of what the shape
  of the traversal gives, not proved of it but met by the 18 units and
  flags of the examples tried: a code id with a use has any usage (the
  rebuild keeps code with a use; the aliases of its calls need any
  usage), a node given arguments has a field read into a node with any
  usage, the sources of what a call reads off its callee with the field
  of known arity are witnesses of known arity of code deps that hold only
  the results of their code dep (with the field of unknown arity:
  witnesses held in that field, with a code id, the same for all calls of
  the callee), and each call has a parameter of its continuation, not of
  kind `Region` or `Rec_info`, for each result. `no_source_arm`: the arm
  reached in a unit that meets every premise, with the least solution:
  code that raises, called with its result used and with no source,
  returning to a parameter unboxed from the allocation that another jump
  gives it; the wrapper is `Invalid`, and the rebuilt unit raises 7 as
  the unit does. The compiler with the fix reaches it too, a type-safe
  program at `-O3` and by default: a `[@loop never]` recursive function
  that raises, whose result Simplify does not make bottom, joined with a
  pair of an abstract type. The arm is taken once and the output is
  unchanged; with the arm made a fatal error, the compiler stops
  ("Inconsistent apply (Unbox ...) and func (Keep ...)", a direct call).
  With `REAPER_INVALIDS=1` it stops too, at the `Option.get` of the
  callee in the message of the `Invalid` (the fix dropped the callee, as
  the code does not use its closure); a variant whose callee has a value
  slot builds and runs.
- The premises of the arm of `make_apply_wrapper` that say what the
  shape of the traversal gives (`FlambdaReaperWrapperShape{Spec,Proofs}.v`).
  Two are proved of the traversal of every unit, with every flag:
  `check_argument_reads` (`traverse_argument_reads`: arguments go only
  to the witness of a call, whose code id is read into `called`, which
  has any usage) and, of a least solution with local fields among the
  slots, `check_used_code_ids` (`traverse_used_code_ids`: a code id is
  held only in the field of the code id of a witness, never aliased
  from, read off or given an argument, and that field is read only into
  a node with any usage, so the one alias from a code id is into such a
  node, and a use of a code id is any usage of it). The proof is an
  invariant of the accumulator along the whole traversal (code, calls of
  both arities, the delayed deps), which needs nothing of the made nodes.
  `arm_needs_no_source_traversal`, `arm_needs_no_source_auto_traversal`,
  `indirect_arm_needs_no_source_traversal` and
  `kept_result_never_unboxed_traversal` are the four statements without
  those two premises. `check_known_witnesses` is proved with `Auto`
  (below); for calls of unknown arity, `check_unknown_widgets` and
  `check_unknown_witnesses` hold of the traversal by inspection but are
  not proved.
  `conts_counted` at toplevel and `plain_kinds` are checks of the term
  already: the arity of each jump and call, which `well_formed_unit`
  does not check and the compiler's terms have, and no handler
  parameter or code result of kind `Region` or `Rec_info`, which the
  rebuild keeps without a use (not checked on the corpus).
- The premise of the arm of `make_apply_wrapper` about the calls of known
  arity, with the flag `Auto` (`FlambdaReaperWrapperAuto{Spec,Proofs}.v`):
  a least solution of the traversal of every unit, with local fields
  among the slots, passes `check_known_witnesses`
  (`traverse_known_witnesses`). The witness of known arity of a code dep
  is made by `prepare_code`, and nothing but
  `create_known_arity_call_witness` puts anything into it (the results
  and the code id of its code, its parameters): no alias goes into it,
  and it is its own one source. The field of known arity of a closure
  holds the witness of a code dep, or a node with any source for code of
  which there is no code dep (`add_closure_dep`); with `Auto` the node
  that a call reads off its callee with that field gets a source only
  from that read (`traverse_widgets_read_only`). The proof is an
  invariant of the accumulator along the traversal, `AutoWidgets` with
  the witnesses of the code deps in place of the widgets; that part holds
  with every flag, and `prepare_code` is the one step that makes a code
  dep. `arm_needs_no_source_auto_end_to_end` and
  `kept_result_never_unboxed_auto_end_to_end` are the statements with the
  flag `Auto` that ask of the unit the fragment of the flow proof,
  `conts_counted` at toplevel and `plain_kinds`, and of the graph a least
  solution and a table of `to_unbox` that passes `check_to_unbox_fixed`:
  the tables of `cannot_change_calling_convention` and of
  `dominated_by_allocation_point` are those derived from the solution
  (`derive_cannot_change`, `derive_dominated`), which pass
  `check_cc_closed` and `check_dominated`. Left: `check_to_unbox_fixed`
  of the derived table (`derive_to_unbox_fixed`, whose rounds of
  `cannot_unbox0` the check asks to be closed), and, for the calls of
  unknown arity (`indirect_arm_needs_no_source`), `check_unknown_widgets`
  and `check_unknown_witnesses`. These hold by the same argument (the
  node that such a call reads off its callee, and each later witness of
  a call with several groups, is only read into; a witness of unknown
  arity of a code dep is the base of the facts of its code only, has a
  code id, and is held in the field of unknown arity of a closure or of
  the node between two witnesses), but need the invariant extended with
  these nodes, whose reads `unknown_arity_apply_deps` makes one group at
  a time.
- The premises of the arm of `make_apply_wrapper` about the calls of
  unknown arity, and the table of `to_unbox`
  (`FlambdaReaperWrapper{Unknown,Derived}{Spec,Proofs}.v`). A least
  solution of the traversal of every unit, with every flag and local
  fields among the slots, passes `check_unknown_widgets`
  (`traverse_unknown_widgets`) and `check_unknown_witnesses`
  (`traverse_unknown_witnesses`). The nodes that a call of unknown arity
  reads with the field of unknown arity, off its callee and, for a call
  of several groups, off the node between two witnesses, are made by the
  call, and nothing but that read puts anything into them, so two read
  off one node have its sources (`widget_any_source_any` and
  `widget_sources_any`: those of `FlambdaReaperLeastProofs.v` for any
  field that is not local). The witnesses of unknown arity of a code dep
  are made by `prepare_code` with the code id of their code, and nothing
  but constructors of them and parameters of which they are the base
  puts anything into them, so each is its own one source; the field of
  unknown arity holds such a witness (the first one of a code dep in a
  closure, the next one between two witnesses) or, for code of which
  there is no code dep, a node with any source. The
  proof is one invariant of the accumulator (`UnknownArity.uinv`), as
  `KnownWitnesses.kinv` with both sets of nodes; `prepare_code` and the
  call of unknown arity are the steps that make them, the latter one
  group at a time (`uinv_apply_deps`). For any tables, the table
  `derive_to_unbox_fixed` passes `check_to_unbox_fixed`
  (`derive_to_unbox_fixed_checked`; `derive_to_unbox_with` passes
  `check_to_unbox_with` for any seeds): each relation of the unboxing
  but `cannot_unbox0` is one pass that gives what its rules conclude,
  and the rounds of `cannot_unbox0` reach the fixpoint, as a round that
  adds something adds an allocation of the sources that was missing and
  there are more rounds than sources.
  `indirect_arm_needs_no_source_auto_end_to_end` is the statement for
  the calls of unknown arity with one group, with the flag `Auto`, the
  derived tables of `cannot_change_calling_convention` and
  `dominated_by_allocation_point`, and a table of `to_unbox` that passes
  `check_to_unbox_fixed`; `arm_needs_no_source_auto_derived`,
  `kept_result_never_unboxed_auto_derived` and
  `indirect_arm_needs_no_source_auto_derived` are the three statements
  with `to_unbox` the derived table: they ask of the unit the fragment
  of the flow proof, `conts_counted` at toplevel and `plain_kinds`, and
  of the graph a least solution only.
- The premises on the term of the statements of
  `FlambdaReaperWrapperDerivedSpec.v`, tested on the corpus.
  `conts_counted`, `unit_counts`, `plain_kinds`, `in_flow_fragment` and
  `projection_free` are extracted (`FlambdaExtract.v`), and `flreaper`,
  where `FLREAPER_WRAPPER_PREMISES` is set, says of each unit whether
  the term given to the model's traversal meets each of them, and where
  a check fails (walks of `flreaper.ml` that follow the definitions, not
  trusted); the expected files do not change. Of the 754 units of
  `tests/semantics/{*,ocaml/*,reaper/*}.fl`, 728 are converted (the 26
  others are not checked, as before). All 728 pass `conts_counted` of
  the continuations of the unit and `plain_kinds`, and the code of each
  passes `conts_counted` of its own continuations. 704 pass
  `in_flow_fragment`; the 24 that fail (8 `heap_*`, 8 `region_cell_*`,
  8 of `ocaml/` with arrays or references) fail for primitives of the
  heap only. 646 are `projection_free`, which the statements ask for
  only where a field is local. `plain_kinds` holds of the converted
  term and not of the compiler's unit in 3 units: `region_param_loop`,
  `region_param_allocated_in` and `region_param_given_after_end` keep,
  after Simplify, a handler parameter of kind `Region` that their text
  writes, which the conversion drops with the regions (decision D37). No
  unit has a handler parameter of kind `Rec_info`, nor code a result of
  either kind, and no unit compiled from OCaml (`ocaml/`) has a
  parameter of kind `Region`. So, on this corpus, the premises on the
  term hold of every converted unit without primitives of the heap, and
  the statements ask nothing more of it than a least solution.
- The repair after the core took the compiler's call kinds
  (`terms/FlambdaModel.v`, `call_kind`: `Direct`,
  `Indirect_unknown_arity`, which was the model's `Indirect`, and
  `Indirect_known_arity`, with or without a list of code ids). The
  files of S7 are in `reaper/`, which `_CoqProject` now names, as
  `rocq/Makefile` does. The transcriptions read a call of known arity
  as the compiler does. The traversal (`traverse.ml`,
  `traverse_call_kind`) gives it the witness of a direct call
  (`make_known_arity_apply_widget`), read off the field of known arity
  of the callee, and no delayed dep, whatever the mode; the code ids
  are not read (`traverse_known_arity_call`). The rebuild
  (`decide_whether_apply_needs_calling_convention_change`:
  `called_call_kind`, `rewrite_call_kind`) makes a direct call or a
  call of known arity whose callee calls one code the direct call of
  it, one whose callee calls several a call of known arity with those
  code ids, and one whose code is not known, if direct, the same call
  with `Auto` and `Always` or for code of another unit, and otherwise
  a call of known arity with no list; it masks the arguments of all
  three as those of a call of known arity (`rebuild_arguments`). A
  call of known arity known to call one code changes its calling
  convention with that code, as a direct call does
  (`code_id_actually_called`), and the rebuild with unboxing has both
  through the functions it shares. Where the compiler stops on an
  empty set of code ids, the call keeps its kind. Before, the rebuild
  made a call of unknown arity where the compiler makes one of known
  arity (a direct call of several code ids, or of code not known with
  `Never`), and a call of known arity of the compiler was read as one
  of unknown arity.
  The proofs follow. The rebuilt call of a direct call is one that
  calls the code of the closure as the direct call does
  (`calls_as_direct`, `shape_of_closure_call_as_direct`): that direct
  call, or a call of known arity that allows the code, with a group of
  arguments at least. The simulation of the unboxing relates two calls
  of known arity whose code is not known as it relates two of unknown
  arity (`closure_call_answered`): the code of the callee keeps its
  arities in both runs, so the tests of the call pass in both or in
  neither. No statement changes but for the name of the call kind.
  Two exclusions of calls of known arity, from `in_flow_fragment` and
  from the checks of the calls of `FlambdaReaperRebuildSpec.v`, were
  lifted by the proofs that followed (the next entry). One condition
  stays:
  - `in_flow_fragment` asks a direct call for one group of arguments
    at least. This one is needed whatever the proofs: the rebuild may
    make a direct call one of known arity (of several code ids, or with
    the mode `Never`), which the machine finds undefined with no group
    of arguments, as the compiler's backend stops on it, where the
    direct call of code with no parameter runs. No direct call that the
    compiler makes has no group: closure conversion makes a call direct
    only where it is full, and its code has a parameter at least.
- Calls of known arity in the statements. `in_flow_fragment` has every
  call of known arity (`apply_in_flow_fragment`): the machine finds
  undefined one that is not a full call of code that is not tupled, and
  that is all the statements need. So do the checks of the calls of
  `FlambdaReaperRebuildSpec.v`, from `direct_tail_calls` to
  `tupled_calls`, which ask of a call of known arity what they ask of a
  direct call, and the checks of the unboxing across calls: `call_ok`
  and `repr_call` take a call of known arity known to call one code as
  a direct call of it (`kind_calls`). The proofs:
  - the flow invariant (`flow_sound` and what is built on it): a call
    of known arity has the edges of a direct call (`known_call_edges`)
    and, where it has a step, is the full call of the code of its
    callee (`full_call_step`), which `direct_call_ok` covers;
  - the rebuild, with and without changes of calling convention
    (`rebuild_refines_observably`, `rebuild_cc_refines_observably` and
    their variants): `direct_answered` answers a direct call and a call
    of known arity alike, the code being that of the callee
    (`direct_like`), whose rebuilt call is the direct call of it or a
    call of known arity that allows it (`rewrite_call_kind_cases`); a
    call of known arity known to call code that changes its calling
    convention is rebuilt as the direct call of it, with the arguments
    it keeps (`rewrite_call_kind_called`);
  - the unboxing across calls (`rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_cc` and
    `rebuild_repr_fixed_refines_cc`, with their variants): a call of
    known arity known to call one code is rebuilt in both runs as the
    direct call of it (`rewrite_call_kind_direct`), and one whose code
    is not known is related as a call of unknown arity is, the code of
    the callee keeping its arities in both runs
    (`closure_call_answered`);
  - the wrapper (`arm_needs_no_source`, `kept_result_never_unboxed` and
    their `_traversal`, `_auto_end_to_end` and `_auto_derived` forms)
    now speak of every call that is not of unknown arity
    (`ap_call_kind ap <> Indirect_unknown_arity`, in place of
    `ap_call_kind ap = Direct id`): a call of known arity reads the
    field of known arity of its callee as a direct call does.
  No statement is false of calls of known arity. The one exclusion
  that stays is of direct calls, and is needed whatever the proofs:
  `in_flow_fragment` asks a direct call for one group of arguments at
  least, as the rebuild may make it a call of known arity, undefined
  with no group (`direct_no_group_counterexample`: with `Auto` and the
  least solution, a direct call with no argument, of code with no
  parameter, whose callee may be a closure of two codes, is rebuilt as
  a call of known arity of the two; the unit exits with 7 and the
  rebuilt unit goes wrong). The compiler's backend stops on such a call
  of known arity, but the compiler makes no direct call with no group
  of arguments, so this is no fault of its reaper.
- The test tool after the call kinds. `flreaper.ml` matches on the three
  kinds: the estimate of the cost of the rebuild counts the groups of
  the calls of unknown arity only, and the walk that says where a unit
  leaves `in_flow_fragment` names a direct call with no group of
  arguments, and a call of known arity, which it can no longer meet.
  The conversion (`flsemantics.ml`) gives the model the compiler's
  `Indirect_known_arity`, with its code ids, so the tool no longer
  reports a call of known arity as a place where the two graphs differ,
  and its header no longer lists it among the known differences. Of the
  examples of `tests/semantics/reaper/`, one expected file changes:
  `cc_known_arity.expected`, where the model now has the compiler's
  graph (75 facts in both, where it had 79), the same solution, the
  same table and decisions of the change of calling convention (the
  code `f` changes, as in the compiler), and the compiler's rebuilt term
  in each mode, so that the section on the fix of the unboxing, which
  is printed only where the fix changes something, is gone. The others
  are unchanged.
  On the corpus (`tests/semantics/{*,ocaml/*,reaper/*}.fl`, 778 units,
  24 more than at the last count), 743 are converted (35 are not
  checked, as before, for reasons of the conversion). All 743 pass
  `conts_counted` and `plain_kinds`; 718 pass `in_flow_fragment`, and
  the 25 that fail do for primitives of the heap only: no unit fails for
  a call of known arity, nor for a direct call with no group of
  arguments. 661 are `projection_free`. Ten units have a call of known
  arity after Simplify (`cc_known_arity`, `stub_by_hand`,
  `known_arity_too_few_arguments`, and seven of `ocaml/`:
  `clos_partial_local`, `compose_chains`, `functor_argument_block_in_slot`,
  `optional_erased_for_higher_order`, `partial_across_join`,
  `partial_exported`, `stub_called_twice`). On all ten the facts of the
  two graphs on the names of the unit, the two solutions, the two tables
  of the code that keeps its calling convention, the decisions of the
  change of calling convention, the three pairs of tables of the
  unboxing, the unboxed fields and the changes of representation are the
  same, and the model's rebuilt term is the compiler's up to bound names
  in the three modes (but for `functor_argument_block_in_slot`, whose
  output the conversion does not take, a closure of deleted code, as
  before). The graphs have as many facts in all but
  `clos_partial_local` (350 in the model, 354 in the compiler), where the
  four of the compiler are on its ends of regions, not on the calls.
  Before, of the nine of these units that the corpus had, eight had more
  facts in the model's graph (a call of known arity read as one of
  unknown arity), five had code that the model kept from changing its
  calling convention where the compiler changed it (`compose_chains`,
  `clos_partial_local`, `partial_across_join`, `cc_known_arity`,
  `optional_erased_for_higher_order`), and four had another rebuilt term
  (`cc_known_arity`, `stub_by_hand`, `optional_erased_for_higher_order`,
  `partial_exported`). Over the corpus, the units where the tables of
  the change of calling convention differ go from 5 to 0, those where
  the solutions differ from 12 to 7, and those where the rebuilt terms
  differ from 17 to 14 (one of them a new example); none of those that
  remain has a call of known arity.
- The fix proposed for the rewriting of types, against the model
  (`FlambdaReaperTypes.v`, `FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v`). Where
  `refine_sets_of_closures` recovers the sources of a closure from its
  usages (`Many_sources_usages`, with a usage that has not any source),
  the fix keeps the sets whose closure at the function slot is among the
  sources recovered, as before, and also those whose closure has any
  usage (`for_known_sources ~include_any_usage:true`); for
  `Single_source` it keeps only the source, as before. That is the rule
  of `fix_refine`, case for case: the model's `for_known_sources` keeps
  the sets whose closure is among the sources, or, where they are
  recovered and the fix is on, whose closure has any usage (`any_usage`,
  the compiler's `PTA.any_usage`). The comment that the fix adds before
  `rewrite_metadata` (a source with any usage may be one of a value
  whose usages it is not a source of) is what `describes` says of
  `Many_sources_usages`. So no statement changes, and only comments do,
  which now name the fix: the statements of the rewriting hold with
  every fix on (`all_fixes`), `fix_refine` among them, and
  `refine_fix_needed` is the compiler's fault without the fix. The
  three other faults are still in the compiler, with the fix and on
  trunk: the fix changes nothing else in `types_rewriter.ml`, and
  `rebuild.ml` and `traversals.ml` are as they were where it matters.
  The rebuild makes a boxed number whose contents have no use poison
  (`reaper_unused_boxed_number`) and `Rewriter.rewrite` keeps its type
  (`Rule.identity`; `fix_boxed`); `rewrite_arbitrary_type` rewrites an
  alias into one to the copy of the name for its metadata, whatever the
  metadata (`get_canonical_with`; `fix_alias`); the closure case makes
  its type with `Expr.at_least_this_closure`, which is not null
  (`fix_null`). A compiler built with the fix runs the reproducer of the
  fault of `refine_sets_of_closures` (the first unit compiled with
  `-O3 -flambda2-reaper -reaper-local-fields
  -flambda2-result-types-all-functions`, the second with `-O3`) as
  without the reaper, printing 16, where one without the fix reaches
  `Invalid`; the test that comes with the fix does the same. Built with
  the fix, the program of two units on an `or_null` closure still
  prints 2 with the reaper, and null without it.
- The fixed mode of the model is now the fix proposed for the reaper
  (`FlambdaReaperUnboxing.v`, mode `fixed`), in place of the earlier fix
  with the flag `unused` on `Keep`. The two seeds of `cannot_unbox0`
  (`fix_seeds`, `check_alias_readers`, `derive_to_unbox_fixed`) are
  unchanged. The rebuild:
  - `make_apply_wrapper_u`: a result that the callee keeps is passed to
    a parameter kept, a parameter kept is given poison for a result that
    the callee deletes or unboxes (`Keep _, Unbox _`, the `Invalid` of
    the branch), and a parameter unboxed for a result that the callee
    keeps or deletes is the fix's fatal error (`Invalid` in the model's
    term, as before; `wrapper_fails_u`). A call that `never_returns`
    is made one that never returns (the wrapper `Invalid`, the model's
    writing of `Never_returns`), whatever the decisions:
    `code_never_returns` (a result of the code dep has no source) for a
    call of code of the unit known to be called, `never_returns_of_call`
    (a result of rank below the arity, at the witness of the last group,
    whose constructor has no source; `false` where a callee or a witness
    has any source) otherwise (`apply_never_returns`).
  - `rebuild_apply_u`: a call of code of the unit known to be called that
    keeps its calling convention gives its arguments at the parameters of
    the code dep with a use (`kept_code_mask`, `kept_arguments_u`; every
    argument for code of no unit); a call whose code is not known keeps
    the mask read off its callee. An unboxed callee there is the fatal
    error "TO CHECK" (`Invalid`, as before).
  - `check_never_returns`: code that `never_returns` whose rebuilt body
    has its return continuation free is a fatal error.
  - The three errors are not in the rebuilt term (which is the same at
    them as before); `rebuild_fails_u` (with `apply_fails_u`,
    `handler_fails_u`, `code_fails_u`) says whether the compiler stops,
    walking the unit as the compiler rebuilds it: the body of every
    `Let` (the compiler rebuilds the body before the binding), a handler
    where the rebuilt body applies it and its parameters are not dead,
    and every code of the unit, used or not (the compiler rebuilds all
    code of `ordered_code_ids`), whose body is rebuilt where its
    parameters are not dead. `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed_fails` is
    the unit's, extracted.
  - `Keep_unused` stays, as the model's mark of a `Keep` whose parameter
    has no use (the first of an exception handler, a result of fixed
    arity with no use); the fix has no flag, and the rebuild treats the
    mark as `Keep` everywhere (`get_args`, `get_args_with_kinds_u`,
    `get_extra_args`, `wrapper_args_u`). The statements read it: the
    relation of the simulation makes such a parameter dead. The branch
    never makes it.
  - The callee of a call under `Keep_my_closure` is kept by the fix
    (`rewrite_simple_opt`); the deliberate difference on a callee the
    earlier fix dropped is gone. New deliberate differences: a call
    whose code is not known and whose callee is a constant gets its
    arguments as they are in the fix, rewritten in the model (as on the
    branch); a call of unknown arity with no argument, a fatal error of
    the fix's analysis, is not one that never returns.
- The variant `tail` of the earlier fix is gone: the fix passes a result
  that the callee keeps, so a tail call from code that keeps its calling
  convention and whose result has no use stays in tail position
  (`tail_example`: the fix rebuilds the unit as the change of calling
  conventions alone does; the regression `fix_tail_call.fl` is gone, and
  its section of the fix is no longer printed). Removed: the section
  variable `tail`, `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_modes` (now
  `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_mode`, of one flag),
  `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed_tail` (no longer extracted),
  `FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSpec.rebuild_unit_unbox_fixed_tail`,
  `TailExample.rebuilt_fixed`, and the conjuncts on the variant in
  `tail_example_statement`, `exn_example_statement` and
  `no_source_arm_statement`. Kept as corollaries, each the statement of
  the fix under its old name: `rebuild_unbox_fixed_tail_refines_cc`,
  `_refines_observably`, `_refines_observably_local`, and
  `rebuild_repr_fixed_tail_refines_cc`, `_refines_observably`,
  `_refines_observably_local`.
- Re-proved for the fix (`FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCalls*`,
  `FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosures*`, the examples of
  `FlambdaReaperUnboxing{Spec,Proofs}` and `FlambdaReaperWrapper*`):
  `rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_cc`, `rebuild_repr_fixed_refines_cc` and
  their `_observably` and `_local` forms. The fragments changed:
  - every call is one that may return (`call_ok`, `ind_call_ok`,
    `repr_call`: `apply_never_returns` or `code_never_returns` is
    `false`); that the call that never returns is sound is part 3;
  - the arguments of a call of code that keeps its calling convention
    (`kept_args_ok`, now of the call): the first rebuild's mask, of a
    call of known arity, and the second's, the code dep's, agree, as
    `masked_args_ok` says;
  - an argument given to a parameter kept with no use (`arg_ok` of
    `Keep_unused`) is a plain name or a constant, or an unboxed name of
    the kind of the parameter, to which the second rebuild gives poison
    of its kind (`rewrite_unboxed_poison`); the rebuild now passes the
    argument where the earlier fix gave poison, and the relation of a
    dead parameter takes either.
  The simulation is otherwise unchanged: the wrapper of the second
  rebuild for a parameter kept with no use is what the variant's was
  (`result_seg` holds of both values of the flag of `wrapper_arg_u`).
  `exn_example`: the code 50 returns its parameter in an arm that is not
  taken, so that its result has a source and the call may return (it
  only raised, and the fix makes such a call one that never returns).
  `no_source_arm`: the call never returns (`apply_never_returns`), the
  rebuilt term is as before, and the fix does not stop
  (`rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed_fails` is `false`).
- `FlambdaReaperTypes*.v` are untouched; their comment that names
  `[_fixed_tail]` is stale.
- Test tool: where the rebuild of the fix, within its budget, would stop
  with one of its fatal errors, `flreaper` prints "with unboxing and the
  fix: the rebuild stops with a fatal error", for every unit. On the
  corpus of `tests/semantics`, no unit does. Of the expected files, only
  `reaper/fix_tail_call.expected` changes (the section of the fix is
  gone: the fix rebuilds the unit as without it); its comment is
  rewritten. The four reproducers still print their sections.
- `make all` (with `claims`), `make assumptions` (the Flambda 2
  statements depend on no axiom; the 167 of the ledger) and the
  semantics tests pass.
  The entries above that this one supersedes:
  - "The unboxing across calls, in the mode of the fix and in that of its
    variant": the variant is gone (see above); "The fix and its variant
    differ only where a call returns to a parameter kept with no use ...
    The regression of the fix is one of space" describes the earlier fix.
    The fragment now also asks that every call may return.
  - "The fixed mode of the model is now the fix of the unboxing that the
    human wrote after the prototype above": the flag `unused` of `Keep`,
    poison for every parameter kept with no use, and the callee dropped
    under `Keep_my_closure { unused = true }` are the earlier fix's; the
    repair of `tail_result` is now by the arm `Keep _, Unbox _` (poison),
    with the same rebuilt unit.
  - "A regression of the fix, found by transcribing it": the fix proposed
    now keeps the tail call (as the "variant" did).
  - "The arm of `make_apply_wrapper` that the fix leaves stubbed": in the
    fix, `Keep _, Unbox _` gives poison, and `Unbox _, (Keep _ | Delete)`
    is a fatal error, after `never_returns`. The statements on the arm
    (`arm_needs_no_source*`, `kept_result_never_unboxed*`) are unchanged
    and hold of the fix's decisions; `arm_pair` still leaves out
    `Keep_unused _, Unbox _`, which `tail_result` reaches and the fix
    gives poison.
- The fatal errors of the fix proposed for the reaper
  (`FlambdaReaperWrapperDerived{Spec,Proofs}.v`, with the flag `Auto`, a
  least solution and the tables derived from it). The fix has three:
  the arm `Unbox _, (Keep _ | Delete)` of `make_apply_wrapper`, after
  `never_returns`; an unboxed callee of a call whose code keeps its
  calling convention (first "TO CHECK", now "Unboxed callee ... cannot
  be applied without changing calling convention"); and
  `check_never_returns`, code that never returns whose rebuilt body has
  its return continuation free. `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed_fails`
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxing.v) says whether one is reached.
  - The unboxed callee is reached (`unboxed_callee_fails_statement`): a
    block, of which a field is read, is called at unknown arity in an arm
    that is not taken. Nothing in the rules forbids to unbox it: a call
    reads the field of the call witness, which is not a real field, and
    the two seeds of the fix need a use with another source or of any
    source. The call, of no known code, keeps its calling convention, and
    the rebuild of the fix stops. The unit is of the flow fragment,
    `projection_free`, `conts_counted` and `plain_kinds`; the tables are
    the least solution and the derived ones; the model rebuilds the call
    as `Invalid`, and the unit and the model's rebuilt unit exit with 1.
    The compiler with the fix reaches it from OCaml in the configuration
    `-Oclassic -flambda2-reaper -X reaper-oclassic=1` (a pair typed as a
    function by a GADT equation in a branch not taken,
    `rbw/tp4/unboxed_callee2.ml` in the scratchpad): "Fatal error:
    Unboxed callee p/53UV cannot be applied without changing calling
    convention." At `-O3` and by default, Simplify makes that call
    `Invalid` (`Closure_type_was_invalid`), as it knows the callee to be
    a block; a block returned by a function that changes its calling
    convention, which Simplify does not see through, is not unboxed by
    the reaper (`unboxed_callee3.ml`), and the call is kept.
  - The arm of `make_apply_wrapper`: not proved. The argument: the
    statements on the arm (`arm_needs_no_source*`) give, for a call that
    the rebuild makes, an arm at the result of rank `i` only where that
    result has no source at the call witness its callee holds
    (`callee_result_has_source`, `result_has_source_at`); for a call of
    code that is known, the witness is that of its code dep, whose result
    of rank `i` is the `i`-th result of the code dep, so that
    `code_never_returns` holds; for a call whose code is not known, the
    sources of the witness that `never_returns_of_call` reads are those
    of the node the call reads off its callee (a closed solution), so
    that the result of rank `i` has no source there and the call
    `never_returns`. In both cases the arm is not reached. What is left:
    (1) a call of code that keeps its calling convention and that expects
    more results than the code dep has (a result beyond the code's never
    has a source, and the code may still return; the compiler makes no
    such call, and a premise on the arity is needed); (2) calls of
    unknown arity with several groups, which the statements on the arm
    do not cover; (3) the walk of `rebuild_fails_u` against
    `unit_rebuilt_calls` (the same decisions; a handler only where it is
    applied; but code that is not used, which the compiler rebuilds and
    `unit_rebuilt_calls` leaves out).
  - `check_never_returns`: not proved. The argument: in code whose
    result of rank `i` has no source, an `Apply_cont` to the return
    continuation is `Invalid` (its argument of rank `i`, of which the
    result is an alias, has no source; a constant is an alias of
    `all_constants`, which has any source; `conts_counted` asks the
    number of arguments), and a call returning there `never_returns`
    (the result of the call is an alias of the result of the code), so
    the return continuation is free in the rebuilt body only through such
    a call that does not. The aliases of the results of a call in code
    are conditional on the code being used; code that is not used, which
    the compiler rebuilds, has its body `Invalid` where a parameter has
    no source, which holds of code that is never called and has a
    parameter (the compiler's code always has one). A model unit with
    code of no parameter could reach the error; the statement needs that
    premise.
  - On the corpus of `tests/semantics`, `flreaper` finds no unit whose
    rebuild with the fix stops (the line is never printed).

- The compiler with the fix (the scratch workspace at the fix, with a
  marker before each of the three errors; boot compiler), on the
  reproducers of `reaper-bugs/`, `repro-wrapper/` (`x.ml`, `x2.ml`),
  `rbw/tailtest/pingpong.ml` and `gaps2/*.ml`, in seven configurations
  (`-O3` without and with the reaper, with local fields, with lambda
  lifting, the default flags, the default flags with the reaper, and
  `-Oclassic` with the reaper): no marker, and every run prints what the
  run without the reaper prints, but one: `gaps2/a5.ml` (a float record
  and a record of values read at one index under a GADT) prints 1 in
  `-Oclassic -flambda2-reaper -X reaper-oclassic=1` where `-Oclassic`
  alone prints 3, with `-no-reaper-unbox` and
  `-no-reaper-change-calling-conventions` too. The reaper rebuilds the
  static float block with `0.` for the field that the float read reads.
  The branch's compiler prints 3. The fix does not touch that code
  (`rewrite_or_variables` of a float block is the same on both), and its
  parent is a commit of main, without the branch's fixes of the reaper:
  most likely the classic float-block miscompile that the branch fixed,
  not a fault of the fix; not checked by building the parent.
  `pingpong.ml` (100,000,000 calls) runs in every configuration: the
  fix keeps the tail call.
- The arm of `make_apply_wrapper` that the fix proposed for the reaper
  stops on (`Unbox _, (Keep _ | Delete)`, `wrapper_fails_u`) is not
  reached by a call that the rebuild makes: the call never returns
  (`apply_never_returns`), which the fix tests first
  (`fix_arm_never_returns_auto_derived_statement`, in
  `FlambdaReaperWrapperDerived{Spec,Proofs}.v`: the flag `Auto`, a least
  solution and the tables derived from it, the unit of the flow
  fragment, `conts_counted` and `plain_kinds`). It covers every call of
  `unit_rebuilt_calls` (the unit and the code that is used) but those of
  unknown arity with several groups of arguments, under one premise on
  the call: a call of code that is known expects of it no more results
  than its code dep has (the compiler's calls do; a call that expects
  more reaches the arm where its code returns).
  - Its two halves (`FlambdaReaperWrapper{Spec,Proofs}.v`, premises of
    `arm_needs_no_source_statement`):
    `fix_arm_never_returns_statement` (a call that is not of unknown
    arity) and `indirect_fix_arm_never_returns_statement` (a call of
    unknown arity with one group of arguments, with
    `check_unknown_widgets` and `check_unknown_witnesses`).
  - The argument. The proof of the statements on the arm now also gives
    a node `a` that the call reads off its callee at the field of its
    arity (`call_ok`, `call_ok_indirect` in the proofs), at which the
    result of rank `i` has no source (`no_source_at`: `a` has no source
    of any source, and no source of `a` holds a result of rank `i` with
    a source). For a call of code that is known, the code's witness is a
    source of such a node (`directly_inv`, `kw_source`,
    `kw_constructor`), and holds the `i`-th result of the code dep
    (`known_witness_edges`), which so has no source:
    `code_never_returns` (`known_never_returns`). Otherwise the callee
    has no source of any source (it would give `a` any source), no
    witness that it holds has a result of any source (`a` would be an
    alias of it), and a result with a source of a witness that
    `never_returns_of_call` reads would be one of `a`, of which `a` is
    an alias, by `alias_accessor_source` and `sources_alias`
    (`one_group_never_returns`); a constant callee gives `a` any source.
  - Not covered: calls of unknown arity with several groups, which the
    statements on the arm do not cover; and code that is not used,
    which the compiler rebuilds too (it rebuilds every code of
    `ordered_code_ids`, used or not, and runs `check_never_returns` on
    it) but `unit_rebuilt_calls` leaves out: the aliases of the results
    of the calls in it hold only where the code is used. Such code is
    never called, so that its parameters have no source and its body is
    `Invalid` (the compiler's `rebuild_body`, and `rebuild_code_u`),
    where the code has a parameter, as all the compiler's code does; this
    is not proved. That the calls `rebuild_fails_u` reaches in code that
    is used are those of `unit_rebuilt_calls`, with the same decisions,
    holds by the definitions (the collector keeps the calls of handlers
    that the rebuild drops, and so has more), and is not proved either.
- `check_never_returns` and the soundness of a call that never returns
  (part 3) are not done; see "Next".
- `check_never_returns`, the third fatal error of the fix proposed for
  the reaper, is not reached by code that is used
  (`check_never_returns_auto_statement`, in
  `FlambdaReaperWrapperDerived{Spec,Proofs}.v`): with the flag `Auto`
  and a least solution, for code of the unit (`unit_codes`) that is used
  and has a result of no source (`code_never_returns`), the return
  continuation is not free in the body that `rebuild_code_u` makes in the
  mode of the fix. It holds of any tables of the rebuild (the
  `cannot_change`, the unboxed fields, the changed representations, the
  first fresh name and the flag of direct calls), not only the derived
  ones.
  - Premises: the unit of the flow fragment (with `projection_free` or no
    local field); its exception handlers well formed
    (`exn_handlers_well_formed`, a condition of `well_formed_unit`: no
    call has the return continuation of code as its exception
    continuation; one that has keeps it free whatever the flow); and two
    of each call of `unit_rebuilt_calls`, which the compiler's terms
    meet: a call of code that is known expects of it no more results
    than its code dep has (the premise of the arm), and a call of unknown
    arity has an argument (`never_returns_of_call` is `false` on no group
    of arguments, the chain of witnesses has one; the compiler's analysis
    stops on such a call). `conts_counted` and the rest of the edges come
    with the code (`code_edges`).
  - The proof (`CheckNeverReturns.no_return`, an induction on the body as
    `calls_ok`, with the continuation, its nodes and one of them with no
    source): an `Apply_cont` to it gives that node an argument of which
    it is an alias, which so has no source, and is rebuilt as `Invalid`
    (`ac_no_source`); a call returning to it never returns
    (`call_never_returns`): the result of the call, read off the witness
    the call reads off its callee, is an alias of what the sources of
    that witness hold, as the code is used. A call of known arity reuses
    `known_never_returns` (through `widgets_same`, every node read off
    the callee has those sources); a call of unknown arity, with any
    number of groups, follows the chain of its witnesses
    (`chain_never_returns`: what `never_returns_of_call` reads at each
    group is among the sources of that witness). Every other term of the
    rebuild keeps the free continuations of its parts, or fewer
    (`fc_unboxed_binding`, `fc_let_u`, `fc_changed_binding`,
    `fc_unboxed_set`, `fc_wrapper`, `fc_apply`, `fc_switch`); the body of
    code binds nothing static. A trap action that names the continuation
    is excluded by the same well-formedness.
  - Code that is not used, which the compiler rebuilds too: not proved.
    The argument: `rebuild_code_u` makes its body `Invalid` if a name of
    `bound` is dead, and `bound` has `my_closure`, of kind `Value`, which
    has no source in such code. Its node is aliased only from the
    `my_closure` of the code dep, which gets sources from a closure of
    the code (a propagate on the code being used) and from a direct call
    of it (an alias from the unit, or a propagate on the caller being
    used); a call that is made makes the code used (its called node has
    any usage and reads the code id off the witness). So the rebuilt body
    is `Invalid`, with nothing free, and the check passes; no premise on
    the parameters is needed (the earlier argument asked for one). A
    proof needs an inversion of the least solution on the facts that
    reach the node of `my_closure`.
  - That the codes the walk of `rebuild_fails_u` checks are those of
    `unit_codes` holds by the definitions, not proved.
  - No statement changed. New: `check_never_returns_auto_statement`. The
    Spec file now imports `FlambdaBindersSpec`; the Proofs file requires
    `FlambdaReaperWrapperProofs`, `FlambdaReaperWrapperShapeProofs`,
    `FlambdaReaperFlowProofs`, `FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs` and
    `FlambdaMapProofs` (already among its dependencies).
  - `make all` (with `claims`) and `make assumptions` pass.
- The rewriting of types, the result types of unboxed variables of code
  for closure types that are not aliases (`FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v` and
  its proofs). The proof of `rewrite_unboxed_result_type_sound` (the
  sections `Destructure` and `Unboxed_sound`) now takes the fragment
  `loose` as a parameter, with `static_sets_whole` where it is `true`,
  as `rewrite_sound` does: it reads the fragment only through
  `rewrite_sound`, `rewrite_forgets` and the lemmas of the fragment,
  which take it already. New statements:
  `rewrite_unaliased_unboxed_result_type_sound` and
  `rewrite_unaliased_unboxed_result_type_sound_local`, the statements of
  unboxed variables with `env_in_fragment_with true` and
  `static_sets_whole`. The old ones are the instance `loose = false`,
  unchanged. The comment of the Spec that named `[_fixed_tail]` now
  names `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed` alone. Nothing found false.

  Left: the closure types of a set whose representation changes, for
  every closure (`changed_example` is of one). Not started; what it
  needs. `rewrite_arbitrary_u` rewrites the closure types of the set and
  the types of the new value slots with itself, so that a statement for
  every closure is one for the whole rewriting, by induction on its
  fuel, of a relation of the plain rebuild to the rebuild with unboxing
  that is `crel` at a closure that changes and recursive below it.
  `crel` gives the new slots what the old ones held in the plain rebuild,
  which is that relation only where no changed closure is stored in a
  block or a value slot (the fragment of the closures simulation, where
  a changed name is not plain); a premise on the graph would say so,
  outside function slots. Then:
  - `rewrite_sound` again, for the composition of `rebuilt_ext` with
    that relation and for a context whose `rc_changed` is not empty
    (`closure_rule` then forgets more): the identity and closure cases
    (`With_rw`, `closure_sound`) read `rebuilt_value` directly and are to
    be generalized over the relation;
  - the case of `changed_rule`: the set kept is that of the closure
    (`refine_keeps`, `sets_of_graph`) and its representation the one
    `crel` has; the index of `exactly_this_closure` (the new function
    slots of the set; the new value slots, as many leaves as
    `fields_leaves`, `cr_fields` being read from the same closures); the
    code ids of the slots called; the closures of the other slots, which
    change too; and the leaves, whose fields are read from the closures
    of the set (`get_fields_of_constructors`, with function slots) and
    not from the uses of a variable, so that `level_ok` and
    `level_metadata_at` are to be redone for them, and `leaves_sound`
    reused below a value slot unboxed in turn.
- The fatal errors of the fix proposed for the reaper, end to end
  (`FlambdaReaperFixFails{Spec,Proofs}.v`, new):
  `fix_fails_only_by_unboxed_callee_statement`. With the flag `Auto`, a
  least solution and the tables derived from it (`derive_cannot_change`,
  `derive_dominated`, `derive_to_unbox_fixed`; any table of
  `to_change_representation` and any flag of direct calls), if
  `rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_fixed_fails` is `true`, some call of

  `unboxed_callee_fails_statement` reaches.

    and `check_never_returns_auto_statement` on the term (the flow
    fragment, with `projection_free` or no local field; `conts_counted`
    at toplevel; `plain_kinds`; `exn_handlers_well_formed`), and three
    new ones:
    - `one_group_unknown_calls`, a check of the term: every call of
      unknown arity has one group of arguments. It stands in for the
      calls of several groups, which the arm does not cover (step 3, not
      done), and gives the premise that such a call has an argument. The
      compiler's calls of unknown arity have a group for each argument,
      so a converted unit with such a call of two arguments fails it.
    - `results_within_codes`, a check of the term: every call that is not
      of unknown arity expects no more results than any code of the unit
      returns (`code_result_kinds`). It gives the premise on the arity of
      the two statements: the code a call is known to call is bound in
      the unit (`unit_codes_names`), and its code dep has as many results
      as its result arity (`code_edges`). A converted unit meets it where
      every code returns at least as many values as every call of known
      arity expects: one value each, but for unboxed products. Not run on
      the corpus.
    - `unused_code_dead`, of the solution, not of the term: the
      `my_closure` of code of the unit that is not used has no source
      (step 2, not done; see below).
  - The walk (step 1, proved, `walk`): where `rebuild_fails_u` is `true`
    at `en`, some call of `rebuilt_calls (ue_decisions en) e` fails
    (`apply_fails_u`), or some code of `unit_codes e` that is used fails
    `check_never_returns`. The decisions are those of `rebuilt_calls`
    (`ue_decisions` is its `ds` at every term); the walk checks a handler
    only where the rebuilt body applies it, and `rebuilt_calls` collects
    every handler; code that is not used stops nothing where its
    `my_closure` is dead (`code_fails_dead`: the first name of `bound`
    is dead, so the body is `Invalid`); and the body of code binds
    nothing static in the flow fragment (`no_statics_codes`), so the
    codes the walk checks are those of `unit_codes`.

    its calling convention, or `wrapper_fails_u` of the decisions of the
    continuation and of the callee (all `Keep`, or the return decisions
    of the change) at a call that does not `never_returns`. The second
    is excluded by `fix_arm_never_returns_auto_derived_statement`; code
    that is used by `check_never_returns_auto_statement`.
  - A gap closed between the walk and the rebuild: `apply_fails_u`

    unknown arity keeps every result, so the arm is a parameter `x` of
    rank `i` of the continuation dominated by an allocation to unbox
    `a0`. `x` has no source of any source, nor has any witness of the
    chain (each would give any source to the next node, the next
    witness, and the last to `x`). At each group, what
    `never_returns_of_call` reads is among the sources with a source of
    the node `a` read with the field of unknown arity (off the callee
    for the first group, off the node the previous witness returns for
    the others), which reaches the witness of the group by aliases. At
    the last group, such a source `z` is held in the field of unknown
    arity of some node and has a code id (`check_unknown_witnesses` at
    `a`), and has a use (the witness has one: its code id is read into a
    node with any usage), so its code is in `cannot_change`
    (`cc_closed_unknown`); a result of rank `i` of `z` with a source
    would be aliased into `x` (`link`), which `cu0_result` forbids for
    code that cannot change its calling convention

    has a source, and the call never returns. `chain_arm_never_returns`
    is `chain_never_returns` (FlambdaReaperWrapperDerivedProofs.v) with
    that conclusion at the last group in place of a parameter with no
    source; `calls_with` is the induction of `calls_ok` for any property
    of a call that holds under the facts of its context.

  closure (`FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v` and its proofs), in the fragment
  where the representations keep no value slot. No fault found.
  `rewrite_changed_type_sound`: of the three runs, the type of the
  fragment `loose` that holds of a closure of the first run at a node,
  under a metadata that holds there, rewritten by `rewrite_arbitrary_u`
  with the changes of representation of the unit
  (`unit_changed_representations` of the table `tcr`), holds of what the
  run of the unit rebuilt with unboxing has for it: the closure, in the
