- Calls whose code is not known that never return: the two shapes that the
  fragment refuses, and the check `used_reach_covered` of the fragments read
  off the unit's own analysis
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverShapes{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin{Spec,Proofs}.v, new).
  - The two shapes are sound in the model, on computed units, and are not
    admitted: the case of such a call in the relation of the proofs is in
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSimProofs.v (`never_targets`,
    `never_closure_answered`, `never_call_answered`, `cont_rel`) and its
    construction in FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs.v
    (`call_rebuilt_never`, `rebuild_uc` on `calls_expr_n`), which this
    change leaves as they are. No choice of the parameters of the relation
    (`RCH`, `NR`, `CS`, `OV`, `REM`) admits them: `flow_ok` puts the code of
    the closure of a partial application in `RCH false f`, of which
    `never_targets` asks no more parameters than the call has groups; and
    the code that an over-application enters first is entered with the
    frame `Over_application`, which `cont_rel_over` relates only with
    related continuations, and `never_closure_answered` asks it to be of
    `NR`.
    - `never_partial_example`: the codes 50 and 51 take two parameters and
      raise the first; the unit applies the closure of one of them (chosen
      by a `Switch`) to 5, a partial application `x32`, and then `x32` to 6,
      which completes it. `never_over_example`: 52 and 53 take one
      parameter and return a closure of 51, which raises its parameter; the
      unit applies the closure of 52 or of 53 to 5 and 6. In both,
      `apply_never_returns` of the fix holds of the call (it follows the
      witnesses of unknown arity group by group, through the chain of the
      partial application and through the first result of the code
      over-applied), `used_never_returns` is 50 and 51, resp. 51, and
      `calls_unboxing_never_nr` and `calls_unboxing_unit_never_nr` refuse
      the unit. The rebuild of the fix gives the call a handler `Invalid`
      and drops the handler 20, which the change of calling conventions
      alone keeps, `Invalid`. The unit, its first rebuild and its rebuild in
      the mode of the fix exit alike (the exception 5, resp. 6), so that the
      rebuilt unit refines the first rebuild (`program_refines`) and the
      unit (`program_refines_observably`); runs computed
      (`exit_alike_refines`).
    - What admitting them needs, in the calls files (after the work on them
      with the heap):
      - the partial application: `never_targets` comparing the parameters
        of the code of the closure of a partial application with the groups
        of the call plus those that the partial application holds, and a
        run invariant giving a lower bound of the latter, carried by
        `flow_ok` (the flow puts a partial application at the allocation of
        the first result of the `k`-th witness of unknown arity of the code;
        `k`, read off the chain of witnesses, is the bound);
      - the over-application: a case of `cont_rel` for an `Over_application`
        whose two continuations are not related, answered in
        `cont_answered` only where what is left of it enters code of `NR` by
        a full call or an over-application; a case of `uc` for such a call,
        whose first code is related at `code_K`; and a run invariant that
        ties what is left of each over-application to the code over-applied
        (`step_rem_ok` ties it to `REM`, the code that what is left of any
        over-application may call, with which the check would ask every
        such code to never return).
  - `used_reach_covered` is not proved in general. Delivered: the statements
    of the `_unit_flow3` and `_unit_never` fragments with no analysis of the
    first rebuilt unit, for the units whose first rebuild only drops facts of
    the traversal (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin*), and, with premises
    on the unit alone, for the units that the first rebuild leaves as they
    are.
    - `facts_within F1 F`: every fact of `F1` is one of `F`, list by list.
      The unit's tables `T` then serve for the first rebuilt unit: the rules
      read the graph only in their premises, so tables closed on `F` are
      closed on facts within `F` (`closed_within`,
      `local_reads_used_within`); the reach, `over_reach` and `rem_reach`
      read the facts only through membership of constructors and of inputs
      `any_source` (`flow_reach2_within`, `over_reach_within`,
      `rem_reach_within`); so the run of the first rebuilt unit is as the
      reach of the unit's own analysis says (`first_runs`, the run part of
      the proof of `rebuild_unbox_flow3_refines_cc`; `flow_run_mono`,
      `over_run_mono`, `rem_run_mono`), which are the run premises of
      `rebuild_unbox_modes_refines_cc_over3` and `_never` for the fragments
      `calls_unboxing_unit_flow3_nr` and `calls_unboxing_unit_never_nr`.
    - New statements, all proved: `within_flow3_refines_cc`,
      `within_flow3_refines_observably`, `within_flow3_observably_local`,
      `within_never_refines_cc`, `within_never_refines_observably`,
      `within_never_observably_local` (the premises of the
      `rebuild_unbox_unit_*` statements, with `in_heap_fragment` and
      `facts_within` of the first rebuild of the unit with its own tables
      in place of `T1`, its two checks and `used_reach_covered`; the
      `_refines_cc` forms ask `check_local_reads_used` of `T`);
      `within_used_reach_covered` (`used_reach_covered F T F1 T e` where the
      constructors and inputs `any_source` of `F1` are among those of `F`,
      `code_facts_within`: the earlier statements then apply with `T` for
      `T1` wherever `T` passes `check_closed` and `check_local_reads_used`
      on `F1`); for units with `settled` (the first rebuild of the unit with
      its tables is the unit), premises of the unit alone:
      `settled_flow3_refines_cc`, `settled_flow3_refines_observably`,
      `settled_flow3_observably_local`, `settled_never_refines_cc`,
      `settled_never_refines_observably`, `settled_never_observably_local`;
      examples `within_example` (the unit of `never_ind_example` is of the
      class, and refines its rebuilt unit by
      `within_never_refines_observably`, with no analysis of the first
      rebuilt unit) and `settled_example` (a unit that applies the closure of
      one of two codes returning their parameter at unknown arity is
      settled and refines its rebuilt unit by `settled_flow3_refines_cc`).
    - Which units are of the class (scratch `vm_compute`): the unit of
      `never_ind_example` and those of the two shapes above (their first
      rebuilds make handler bodies `Invalid` and list the code of a call of
      known arity, which adds no fact); not those of `flow_example`,
      `flow2_example`, `flow3_example` (changes of calling convention) nor
      that of `poison_reach` (poison). Settled units are few: the first
      rebuild is not idempotent (that of the first rebuild of the unit of
      `never_ind_example` drops the parameters of the handlers made
      `Invalid` and adds a wrapper).
    - What blocks the rest:
      - the traversal numbers fresh nodes (`Node_made`) in turn, so a
        rebuild that drops a call, a primitive or code renumbers the fresh
        nodes of what follows; a renaming of fresh nodes between the two
        traversals is needed, with the unit's tables renamed by it;
      - poison given to a parameter with no use adds an argument
        `Node_all_constants` (any source): the unit's tables have to be
        given an alias to it and any source at such parameters and at what
        they flow to, which needs that a node with no use flows only to
        nodes with no use (through `alias`, accessors and arguments; to be
        checked against each of the 45 rules, the guarded ones included);
      - a call made direct adds the facts of the known-arity widget and,
        with `Auto`, two fresh nodes, aliases under any source of the callee
        and an apply dep; a change of calling convention adds wrappers and
        renumbers parameters: their facts have to hold in the (renamed)
        unit's tables;
      - a comparison of least solutions instead runs into the negative
        guards (`s_any_source`, `s_any_usage`), as the earlier entry says.
      With these, `facts_within` would become an inclusion up to renaming
      and saturation, covering units with dead code, unused parameters and
      changes of calling convention.
  - Premises: none added to existing statements. The new statements take,
    beside the premises on the unit, `in_heap_fragment` and `facts_within`
    of the first rebuild with the unit's tables (or `settled`), in place of
    the tables `T1`, their two checks and `used_reach_covered`.
  - Nothing found false.
  - No existing file changed but the Makefile, which lists the four new
    files.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 740 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 17
    new) passes; the statements of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverShapesSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithinSpec.v depend on no axiom outside the
    ledger (`own-assum.sh`: 17 statements, 17 closed under the global
    context).
- The fix proposed for the compiler for bug 12 (the code age relation
  bridged over the code that changes), the cut of the relation found not
  to be a fix, and two versions of a code that the unboxing treats
  differently
  (FlambdaReaperCodeAgeSpec.v and FlambdaReaperCodeAgeProofs.v, extended;
  FlambdaReaperCodeAgeVersionsSpec.v and
  FlambdaReaperCodeAgeVersionsProofs.v, new).
  - The cut of the relation (`cut_relation`) is not a fix of bug 12.
    `rebuild_cc_cut_keeps_conventions_statement` stays true: the
    conventions agree for the cut relation in the rebuilt unit. But the
    cut drops links that still hold: with three versions `a`, `b`, `c`
    (`c` the newest) where only `b` changes its calling convention, it
    drops both links, though `a` and `c` keep their conventions and `c`
    is still a newer version of `a`. A client's direct call naming `a` of
    a closure of `c`, which runs on the unit, is undefined on the rebuilt
    unit with the cut relation (`cut_loses_link_statement`). The head of
    FlambdaReaperCodeAgeSpec.v, the comment of `cut_relation` and that of
    `conventions_fixed_statement` now say so; they presented both fixes
    as repairing the unit of the bug. Also corrected: the comment of
    `rebuild_cc_keeps_conventions_statement` said "no code that a link
    relates is in the table" where its premise is that every one is.
  - The fix of the compiler (`rewrite_newer_version_of`,
    `reaper/unboxing_analysis.ml`): code that changes its calling
    convention gets no older version; code that keeps it gets the first
    code down its chain that keeps its convention or is not of the unit.
    In the model: `keeps_convention` (the test of the rebuild with
    changes of calling convention: not a code of the unit, in the table,
    or no code dep), `bridge_walk` (with fuel; the walk of the compiler
    has none, and ends on relations without cycles, as those of Simplify
    are) and `bridge_relation` (the fuel the length of the relation).
    - `rebuild_cc_bridge_keeps_conventions_statement`: the conventions
      agree for the bridged relation in the rebuilt unit where they agree
      for the relation in the unit. One premise: every code with a code
      dep is a code of the unit, so that the codes passed over, which
      change, are codes of the unit along whose chain the conventions
      agree; the two ends keep theirs. No premise on the relation: the
      walk stops at its fuel. No case was found where the rule breaks
      the conventions.
    - `cut_in_bridge_statement`: the cut is contained in the bridged
      relation, for a test of the bridge that holds wherever that of the
      cut does. The bridge has besides a link from each kept code to the
      first kept code down its chain, where the cut has none.
    - `conventions_bridge_statement`: on the unit of bug 12 (`f51`
      changes) the bridged relation is empty, as the cut is.
    - `cut_loses_link_statement` (`ChainExample`): `f52`, `f51`, `f50`,
      each a newer version of the next, all of two parameters returning
      the first and reading nothing of their closure; the closures of
      `f50` and `f52` escape, the static closure of `f51` is called
      directly only, and `f51` alone loses a parameter. The unit meets
      the premises of the statement of the rebuild for the least
      solution and does not go wrong; its rebuild refines it. The
      conventions agree in the unit, not in the rebuilt unit for the
      relation (bug 12); the cut is empty; the bridge is `f52 -> f50` and
      the conventions agree for it. The relation is sound for the closure
      `s81` of `f52` in the unit, and the client's call naming `f50`
      exits with 5, refined by the call naming `f52`. In the rebuilt
      unit, with the cut, the call naming `f50` is undefined; with the
      bridge it exits with 5, the relation is sound for `s81`, and the
      call naming `f52` refines it.
  - The compiler's fix (proposed, not in main) applies this rule to both
    copies of the relation: the `newer_version_of` of the code metadata
    and the code age relation of the typing environment, which the
    `.cmx` exports and which clients read through
    `code_age_relation_resolver`. With it, the reproducer of bug 12
    exports no link from the copy whose unused parameter was deleted,
    and the links it keeps relate codes of one convention (run on
    2026-11-27).
  - Two versions that the unboxing treats differently
    (FlambdaReaperCodeAgeVersionsSpec.v). Within the unit, the rebuild
    makes no call mix them: at a direct call whose callee has a source
    it names the code that the flow finds, the code of the closure, with
    that code's convention and representation; at one whose callee has
    any source it keeps the code named, whose `my_closure` then has any
    source, which forbids the change of representation of its sets. The
    compiler does the same (`rebuild.ml`,
    `decide_whether_apply_needs_calling_convention_change`;
    `unboxing_analysis.ml`, `cannot_change_representation0`), and reads
    `newer_version_of` nowhere but a CR of `types_rewriter.ml`. What is
    not kept is a client's call.
    - `rebuild_unbox_sound_conventions_statement`: with the table of the
      fix (`age_fixed_table`), every rebuild with unboxing
      (`rebuild_unit_unbox_repr_mode_with`: each mode, with or without
      types, any changes of representation; the rebuilds of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefines*Spec.v are instances) keeps the
      conventions of the linked codes in the statics of a unit that
      begins with its `Let_static`: they meet `sound_conventions_for` for
      every closure where the conventions agree in the unit. So no
      parameter of a linked code is unboxed or deleted. (Only the codes
      of the first `Let_static` are looked at, which needs no induction
      on the rebuild.)
    - `repr_client_counterexample_statement`: the table does not reach
      the changes of representation. Codes 53 and 55, 55 a newer version
      of 53, read the value slot 80 of their closure at the function
      slot 70; the closure `x60` of 53 is called directly; the static
      closure `s90` of 55 escapes, and the unit calls it directly naming
      53. With the table of the fix (which passes the check) no code
      changes its calling convention, but the set of `x60` changes its
      representation (slot 81, function slot 82) and 53 reads the new
      slots. The unit goes wrong on `step`; on the machine it exits with
      `[7; 7; s90]`, and so does the rebuilt unit, which names 55 at the
      call of `s90` and refines the unit. The conventions agree in the
      unit and in the rebuilt unit, and the relation is sound for `s90`
      in both (in the rebuilt unit 53 with `s90` is undefined). A
      client's call of `s90` naming 53 exits with 7 with the statics of
      the unit and is undefined with those of the rebuilt unit, which
      does not refine it; naming 55 it exits with 7, and with no change
      of representation the call naming 53 exits with 7.
    - Not reached: a fix stated for every unit (leave out of the changes
      of representation the sets of a code that the relation links to a
      newer version, or rename the slots of the newer versions' closures
      alike), which would need a statement of what a client may call.
      Not attempted: a reproducer in the compiler. Its reaper changes the
      representation of every set it can (`to_change_representation`),
      with new function and value slots, but a client names the older
      code of a closure of the newer only where a type says so, and the
      types of Simplify name the code of the allocation (the join of two
      code ids is unknown, their meet the newer).
  - Tools (FlambdaReaperCodeAgeProofs.v): `conventions_agree_b` and
    `conventions_agree_of_b`, `code_deps_bound_of_check` (checks
    computed once, in place of case analysis on computed records, which
    made the kernel's check of one proof grow to several gigabytes);
    FlambdaReaperCodeAgeVersionsProofs.v: `compute_steps`, and the runs
    of a client stated with `run_by` where the kernel compares them with
    `refines_by`, which otherwise runs them again (a Qed of minutes).
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 746 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 6
    new) passes; `own-assum.sh` on FlambdaReaperCodeAgeSpec,
    FlambdaReaperCodeAgeUnboxingSpec and FlambdaReaperCodeAgeVersionsSpec:
    81 statements, all closed under the global context.
- The plain rebuild on units with regions, on the machine of regions of
  the core, through its erasure (FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v, proved in
  FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.v with the lemmas of
  FlambdaReaperRegionsRebuildProofs.v, FlambdaReaperRegionsTraverseProofs.v
  and FlambdaReaperRegionsBaseProofs.v).
  - The fragment `regions_plain`: no binder of kind `Region` but a begin
    (no region parameter of a handler or code, no call result of that
    kind); begins and ends as the conversion applies them; a
    `Local_allocation` directly after the `Let` of an allocation of the
    variable that it binds again, at the same kind; no variable of a region
    evaluated but by an end or a `Local_allocation`; no primitive of
    regions among the projections of a set of several closures. The
    conversion makes all but the last of every unit without a region
    parameter (three units of `tests/semantics` keep one after Simplify,
    none compiled from OCaml).
  - Commutation, proved for any tables
    (`rebuild_erase_commutes_statement`): on the fragment, `erase` of the
    rebuilt unit is the rebuild of `erase` of the unit, with the facts of
    the traversal of the erased unit, the same tables, and the first fresh
    name of the unit with regions (the erased unit's may be smaller; it
    only names call wrappers). The two traversals have the same
    constructors, accessors, parameters and arguments (all the rebuild
    reads of the facts); otherwise the erased graph lacks the facts of the
    regions and has an alias to the constants of each end variable, which
    the erasure binds to the unit value.
  - Refinement (`least_rebuild_regions_refines_statement`): for a least
    solution of the graph of the unit with its regions (what the compiler
    analyses), with the premises of `least_rebuild_refines_observably` on
    `erase [] e`, `e` erasable with fresh symbols, and the rebuilt unit
    erasable, with fresh symbols, and passing `region_check` (any
    certificate), the rebuilt unit refines `e` on the machine of regions
    (`regions_refine_observably`): an exit of `e` is one of the rebuilt
    unit, the values erased (`value_erased`) to values observed equal; an
    exit of the rebuilt unit is one of `e` alike, or `e` stops without a
    step, with or without regions; the rebuilt unit stops without a step
    only where `erase [] e` goes wrong. Proof: the least solution with an
    alias of each variable with any source to the constants, which then
    have every usage, is closed for the erased graph (no fact of any
    unit's traversal puts something into the constants, and a least
    solution has no alias from them); the base refinement for any first
    fresh name and a solution given as propositions
    (`rebuild_refines_observably_gen`, the proof of
    `rebuild_with_refines_checked_local` with those left free); the
    commutation; `erasure_exits_erased`, `erasure_adds_no_exit_erased` and
    `region_check_sound` of the core.
  - Example (`regions_example_statement`): a region with two local
    blocks, one unused; the unit meets every premise, its rebuild drops
    the unused block and its `Local_allocation`, and both exit with 7 on
    the machine of regions.
  - Found false, model artefacts, both the known differences:
    - `boxed_poison_counterexample_statement`: a local boxed number whose
      contents nothing reads (it is read by `Is_int` in a handler that
      nothing applies). The unit meets the premises, the commutation holds,
      but the rebuild keeps the `Local_allocation` after the poison: the
      rebuilt unit has no step there (`Not_of_the_conversion`) where the
      unit exits with 0, fails `region_check` with every certificate, and
      does not refine the unit on the machine of regions. rebuild.ml
      replaces the whole `Box_number`, mode included, by poison.
    - `local_set_counterexample_statement`: a local set of two closures
      whose second closure has no use. The rebuild with regions removes
      its projection (`projections_of_set` does not look past the
      `Local_allocation`, where the traversal does), the rebuild of the
      erased unit keeps it: the commutation fails; both rebuilt units exit
      as the unit. rebuild.ml keeps every closure of a set it keeps.
  - Not reached: the rebuild with changes of calling convention and with
    unboxing (the commutation of `rebuild_expr_cc` and of the unboxing
    rebuild, and the transfer of their tables, `derive_cannot_change` and
    `derive_to_unbox`, to the erased graph); that the rebuild keeps
    `erasable` and the freshness of symbols (premises, believed to hold on
    the fragment).
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 751 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 5 new)
    passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v depend on no
    axiom outside the ledger (own-assum.sh: 5 statements, 5 closed under
    the global context).
- The unboxing across calls and code with the primitives of the heap
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsHeap{Spec,Proofs}.v, new; the
  simulation `uc` of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSimProofs.v and its
  construction in FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs.v extended in
  place). No counterexample: the statements of the calls fragment hold of
  the units of `in_heap_fragment`.
  - How the unboxing across calls meets the heap, as the local unboxing
    does: what `calls_unboxing` unboxes is bound to an immutable block, a
    boxed number, an alias or the read of a field unboxed in turn, or is
    a parameter given such a name, never a cell; an unboxed name, a
    parameter included, is no argument of a primitive
    (`plain_binding_ok`); a field of an unboxed block may hold a
    location, which the variable of the field holds, is given across a
    call or returned as any other field, and the read of the field
    becomes. A parameter kept with no use, to which the second rebuild
    may give poison, is no argument of a primitive either (both rebuilds
    make poison of a name with no use where they read it). The two
    rebuilds make the same primitives of the heap on the same values, so
    the heaps are one, and the comparison against the change of calling
    conventions is `program_refines`.
  - The route: `uc` extended in place, not copied as `ubh` is for `ub`.
    The simulation and its construction are about 15000 lines, and the
    heap enters them only at the `Let`s and through the state: one new
    case, `uc_let_heap` (a `Let` of both, of any primitive, with
    arguments the same in both runs, the variable plain), and the states
    related have one heap (`related_main`, `related_call`,
    `related_pending` ask `state_heap t = state_heap s` in place of both
    empty). The lemmas of the simulation that mention the heap (17:
    `cont_rel_steps`, `closure_answered`, `call_code_same`,
    `closure_call_answered{,_r,_g}`, `ind_call_answered{,_k,_1,_f,_o,_c}`,
    `run_call_no_exit`, `cont_answered`, `call_code_never`,
    `never_closure_answered`, `never_call_answered`) are now proved of any
    heap of the two runs (`X_hp (hp0 : heap)`), and each old lemma keeps
    its name and type as the instance at the empty heap. In
    `main_answered`: the `Let` of both keeps the heap the step gives; an
    unboxed allocation is of a block or a boxed number, which reads no
    heap (`alloc_field_not_reading`, `exec_named_keeps`); a read of an
    unboxed variable runs on a heap as without one
    (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesHeapProofs.read_exec). `uc_refines_flow`
    and `uc_refines` keep their types (the initial heaps are both empty).
  - The construction: `rebuild_ok_expr_h`, `code_rebuilt_nc_h`,
    `code_rebuilt_h`, `bindings_rebuilt_h`, `rebuild_uc_h` hold for the
    edges of terms with the primitives of the heap if `hprims` (the
    `Let` of a variable not unboxed is related by `uc_let_heap`, through
    the new `uc_result_let_heap`, whatever its primitive); the old lemmas
    are the instances at `False`. `uc_agree` and `uc_never_ext` have the
    new case. `rebuild_unbox_modes_with_refines_cc_never_gen`,
    `rebuild_unbox_modes_refines_cc_never_gen` and
    `rebuild_unbox_modes_with_refines_cc_gen` take the fragment with the
    primitives of the heap if `hb` (the edges by
    FlambdaReaperFlowProofs.traverse_unit_facts_gen); the old lemmas are
    the instances at `false`. The same for the calls whose code is not
    known that never return: `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind_refines_cc_gen`
    (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverProofs.v) and
    `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind_with_types_cc_gen`
    (FlambdaReaperTypesProofs.v), whose flow is read off the first
    rebuilt unit, which the statements ask of `in_heap_fragment` already;
    the old theorems are the instances at `false`.
  - New statements, all proved, of the units of `in_heap_fragment`:
    - Against the change of calling conventions, heaps equal:
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_cc_heap`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_cc_heap_never_returns` (code `nr`
      taken to never return).
    - Against the unit, up to a correspondence of locations
      (`program_refines_up_to_heap`), composed with
      `rebuild_cc_refines_up_to_heap{,_local}`:
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_up_to_heap{,_local}`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_up_to_heap_never_returns`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_heap_local_never_returns`.
    - With types, composed with
      `rebuild_cc_with_types_refines_up_to_heap_local`:
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_with_types_cc_heap_nr`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_with_types_heap_local_nr`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_with_types_up_to_heap_nr`.
    - Calls whose code is not known that never return (the fragments of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverSpec.v and
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesUnitFlowSpec.v), without and with
      types: `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind_{cc_heap,heap_local,up_to_heap}`,
      `rebuild_unbox_unit_never_{cc_heap,heap_local,up_to_heap}`,
      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind_with_types_{cc_heap,heap_local,up_to_heap}`,
      `rebuild_unbox_unit_never_with_types_{cc_heap,heap_local,up_to_heap}`.
    - `calls_heap_example` (`CallsHeapExample`, computed): the code 50
      takes a block holding a cell, reads the cell from it and stores 2
      into it; the unit makes the cell `x10` and the block `x11`, calls 50
      directly with `x11` and returns what the cell holds. Of
      `in_heap_fragment` and not of `in_flow_fragment`; with the least
      solution, the rules of the fix unbox `x11` and the parameter `x40`
      (the cell, stored into, has any use and is not unboxed); of
      `calls_unboxing`; the rebuild in the mode of the fix gives 50 the
      field of `x11`, the location, as its parameter 61, which the read
      becomes; the unit and the rebuilt unit both exit with 2 and the same
      heap; the rebuilt unit refines the first rebuild (heaps equal) and
      the unit up to the heap.
  - Premises added: none beyond `in_heap_fragment` in place of
    `in_flow_fragment`, and, for the statements up to the heap, the
    premises of the statements up to the heap of the change of calling
    conventions, which are those up to observation.
  - Existing names and types are unchanged. Changed in meaning: the
    inductive `uc` has one more constructor (`uc_let_heap`, last of the
    cases of `uc`, so the scheme `uc_mut` has one more case after that of
    `uc_call_ind_never`), and `related_main`, `related_call`,
    `related_pending` relate states with one heap, not only the empty one
    (one conjunct, `state_heap t = state_heap s`, in place of two). A
    proof that destructs `uc` or those relations needs the new case or
    pattern.
  - Left: the closures fragment with the heap (the simulation `rr`), the
    unboxing of cells (`cell_example`), and the other fragments of the
    calls (Over to Reach3) stated apart with the heap: they are held by
    that of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverSpec.v, as without the heap.
  - The example's proof applies the statements to the graph and the code
    deps as the statements name them (`traverse_unit ..`,
    `unit_code_deps ..`, rewritten from `CallsHeapExample.facts` and
    `code_deps` by equalities proved by `reflexivity`): unifying
    `calls_unboxing CallsHeapExample.facts ..` with
    `calls_unboxing (traverse_unit ..) ..` directly made the file take
    more than three hours; it takes seconds.
  - Nothing found false.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 773 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 22
    new) passes; the statements of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsHeapSpec.v (22), and those of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesUnitFlowSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperTypesSpec.v, whose proofs changed, depend on no axiom
    outside the ledger (`own-assum.sh`: all closed under the global
    context).
- The result types at the results of code, the fragment `loose` for the
  rebuild with changes of calling convention and the runs after it, and
  static blocks whose fields are not all values
  (FlambdaReaperTypes{Returns,Loose,UsedValues}{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperTypesCcSetsProofs.v, new;
  FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.v and the header of
  FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v changed). The statements hold; beyond the
  wider check of static blocks the statement of exported types is false
  of the model (a counterexample, stated), not of the compiler.
  - What was in the way. The model's traversal gives the results of code
    made nodes (`cd_return`), and FlambdaReaperTypes.rewrite_result_type
    reads the metadata of a variable, so the statements of result types
    were of the variables that the run binds. The statements of the
    fragment `loose` ask that the static sets be kept whole by the runs
    they relate (`static_sets_whole`), which FlambdaReaperTypesProofs.v
    proved of the plain rebuild only. The check `unit_of_values` asks
    every static block to hold values, where the compiler reads a static
    block at its shape.
  - The route.
    - Results of code. The compiler gives `rewrite_result_types` the
      variables of the code dep (unboxing_analysis.ml: `code_dep.params`,
      `code_dep.return`), which `Traverse.prepare_code` makes with
      `Variable.create`; the model's traversal makes a made node for each,
      so the node of the result of rank `i` is that of rank `i` of
      `cd_return` (`result_node`), and its type is rewritten under the
      metadata of that node (`rewrite_result_type_at`, of which
      `rewrite_result_type` is the instance at the node of a variable,
      by definition). A return of the code is an `Apply_cont` of its body
      to its return continuation not under a binding of it
      (FlambdaReaperDeadFramesSpec.applies_to, the arms of a switch kept
      among them): the body has the edges of the flow at the results of
      the code dep (FlambdaReaperWrapperProofs.unit_codes_edges), which
      alias the node of the result to the argument
      (FlambdaReaperDeadFramesProofs.applies_to_edges); the symbols of a
      return are noted (`applies_to_noted`) and so allocations of their
      own (`return_alias`, as FlambdaReaperDeadFramesProofs.unit_inv).
      What the rebuild relates at the node of the argument it relates at
      every alias of it (`rebuilt_value_alias`), and the type is
      rewritten at the node as FlambdaReaperTypesProofs
      .rewrite_result_type_sound_in rewrites it at a variable
      (`rewrite_result_type_at_sound_in`).
    - The sets kept whole. FlambdaReaperTypesCcSetsProofs.v: the proofs of
      the section `Rebuilt_sets` of FlambdaReaperTypesProofs.v, of
      FlambdaReaperCallingConventions.rebuild_expr_cc (bindings rebuilt
      as the plain rebuild's but code; calls wrapped in a handler that
      applies a continuation): the rebuilt unit of a well-formed unit
      binds its static sets whole wherever it runs to
      (`cc_rebuilt_sets_bound`). With FlambdaReaperTypesProofs
      .statics_rel_whole on the static environments that the simulation
      relates, FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.cc_states now gives
      `static_sets_whole` of the states it relates (a conjunct added at
      its end), and `calls_states`, `calls_exit` and `repr_exit` give it
      of the third run (its static values are those of the second,
      `static_sets_whole_values`); the uses of these lemmas are adjusted.
    - The wider check. The statements of exported types read the static
      values of the symbols with a use only (`statics_rebuilt`), and the
      rebuild keeps the binding of a static block exactly where its
      symbol has a use (`static_const_is_used`). `used_values T e`: the
      check of FlambdaReaperTypesTermSpec.v asked of the static blocks
      whose symbol has a use in the solution only
      (`unit_of_values_with`, of a predicate `used`). The invariant of
      the run of FlambdaReaperTypesTermProofs.v is that of `used`
      (`state_of_values_with`); FlambdaReaperTypesProofs
      .statics_rel_rebuilt reads the fragment of the static values of the
      symbols with a use only (`statics_rel_rebuilt_used`, its proof).
  - New statements, all proved:
    - FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsSpec.v (10):
      `code_result_type_end_to_end` (plain rebuild) and
      `cc_code_result_type_end_to_end` (rebuild with changes of calling
      convention), each with `_landed`, `_local`, `_local_landed`: at

      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesSpec.CallExample, whose code 50

      `cc_unaliased_result_type_end_to_end`,
      `unaliased_code_result_end_to_end`,
      `cc_unaliased_code_result_end_to_end` (the results of code),

      `calls_unaliased_result_end_to_end`,
      `calls_unaliased_unboxed_end_to_end` (the unit rebuilt with

    - FlambdaReaperTypesUsedValuesSpec.v (18):
      `unit_of_values_with_runs` (the static blocks of the symbols that

  - Premises added, and why. For the results of code, that the node of
    the result has a use (the rebuild makes poison of an argument with
    no use, and the change of calling conventions deletes a result with
    no use, whose type the compiler drops), and the premises of

  - Left:
    - The results of code for the unit rebuilt with unboxing across calls:

    - The parameters at the nodes of `code_dep.params`, as the compiler

  - Found false, of the model and not of the compiler:
    `naked_unread_counterexample` (and `_landed`, `_compiler`, of the

    `let symbol s50 = { 7; #3 } in let x10 = s50.(0) in return x10`, a
    static block of the shape of a record of a value and a naked
    immediate, with its least solution, meets every premise of

    `Static_const.block_field_kind`), its rebuild makes it poison of that
    kind (rebuild.ml, `rewrite_static_const`, by
    `K.Scannable_block_shape.element_kind`), and its rewriting reads the
    field at the kind of its type (types_rewriter.ml, `block_slot`), the

    static blocks with their shapes would lift the check.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 835 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 62
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperTypesLooseSpec.v, FlambdaReaperTypesUsedValuesSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v (whose proofs changed) depend on no
    axiom outside the ledger (`own-assum.sh`: 88 statements, 88 closed
    under the global context).
- The closure types of the sets whose representation changes applied end
  to end to the runs of the simulation of the changes of representation
  (`rr`), on the fragment `repr_calls_unboxing_nr`
  (FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosures{Spec,Proofs}.v, new; the relation of
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresSimProofs.v and a lemma of
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresProofs.v extended; an example added
  to FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresReach{Spec,Proofs}.v). No
  counterexample: the statements hold; the existing statements are
  unchanged in name, type and meaning. Of the milestones of the round
  (the exact slots and the closure types end to end; wrappers of calls
  that return; over-applications at unknown arity; unboxed parameters;
  changed closures in fields and static sets), the first is done; the
  others are not started (see Left).
  - What was in the way.
    - `crel` of `rr` gave only that each new value slot holds the value at
      its path; the closure-type statements ask `changed_closure_rebuilt`
      of the third run's closure: the new value slots of the
      representation and no other, in their order, each holding a value
      of the kind of its leaf. `crel` itself could not change:
      FlambdaReaperTypesProofs.changed_example_crel builds it with its four
      conjuncts.
    - The kind: the leaf of a value slot not unboxed in turn has the kind
      `Value` (FlambdaReaperUnboxing.field_kind), but the model's value
      slots have no kind and may hold a naked number; nothing in the
      simulation gives the kind of what such a slot holds.
    - `changed_at` asks the representation of every allocation that the
      node of the variable may come from, not of the variable: for a
      `my_closure`, which a closure of the set dominates, that of the
      closure.
    - `static_sets_whole` of the second and third runs, which the
      statements of the fragment `loose` ask, is proved of the plain
      rebuild only.
    - The names of `rr` (`M`, `CH`) were existential in
      `rebuild_repr_reach_modes_with_rr`, so a changed variable of the
      unit could not be read as changed in the relation.
  - The route.
    - `crel_exact` (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresSimProofs.v):
      `crel`, and the value slots of the closure of the second run of `rr`
      are exactly the leaves of the representation (`map fst vs' = map fst
      (fields_leaves (cr_fields r))`, no key twice), the fields of the
      representation are value slots, and the closure of the first run is
      at a function slot of its set. `env_rel_c` and `class_rel` use it in
      place of `crel`; it is made at the `Let` of a set that changes from
      what `rr_set` already asks (`set_crel_exact`: `new_slots_keys_c`,
      `eval_value_slots_keys`). `crel` is unchanged.
    - `rebuild_repr_reach_modes_with_rr_names`
      (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresProofs.v): the relation of the
      two rebuilds with its names those of the unit (`M'` and `CHf` of
      the unboxed fields and the changes of representation of the unit);
      `rebuild_repr_reach_modes_with_rr` keeps its name and type and is
      derived from it.
    - FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v: `crel_exact_rebuilt`
      (`crel_exact` and the kinds of the new slots not unboxed in turn give
      `changed_closure_rebuilt`); `dinv` (a name with a representation is
      dominated by an allocation with the same one, kept by the fold of
      `changed_representations` beside
      FlambdaReaperTypesTraversalProofs.rinv) and `changed_at_of` (with
      `check_dominated`, that allocation is the only one of the name);
      `rr_blocks` (`rr` binds static blocks only, so the second run binds
      no static set and `static_sets_whole` asks nothing,
      `sets_whole_of_blocks`); `repr_runs` (the three runs, read as
      FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.cc_states and with `rr_reach_blocks`,
      the third well-kinded by FlambdaWellKindedProofs.run_well_kinded);
      the statements are FlambdaReaperTypesTraversalUnaliasedProofs
      `.changed_type_sound_full` and `.stored_type_sound_full` applied to
      them (`held_rc` for the variables to which nothing that changes
      comes, `rebuilt_changed_of_runs` for those that change).
  - New statements (FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresSpec.v), all proved:
    - `repr_changed_types_end_to_end_statement` and `_landed`: at a state of
      the run of the unit (which does not go wrong) that binds a variable
      `x` of the unit, with a use, whose representation `r` changes (the
      variable of a set, or a name that a closure of the set dominates, a
      `my_closure`), the run of the unit rebuilt with changes of calling
      convention binds `x` to `v_mid`, related by `rebuilt_value`, and the
      run of the unit rebuilt with unboxing and changes of representation
      binds `x` to `v'` with `changed_closure_rebuilt r v_mid v'`; the
      closure type of the fragment `loose` that holds of the value of `x`,
      rewritten with the changes of representation under a metadata that
      holds at `x`, holds of `v'`.
    - `repr_stored_types_end_to_end_statement` and `_landed`: the same for
      any variable of the unit with a use that changes, or that the

      value in the third run.
    - `repr_types_example_statement` and `_landed` (`ReprTypesExample`,
      computed): FlambdaReaperUnboxing.Examples.block_in_slot (the closure
      `x60` of the code 52 holds in its slot 80 the block of 7 and 8, whose

      80 unboxed into the new slot 81, function slot 82) meets every

      `check_changed_unstored_sound`; the rebuilt unit well-kinded); the

      which the rewritten type holds.
  - Premises added, and why.
    - `repr_kinds_premises`: the unit rebuilt with unboxing and changes of
      representation is well-kinded
      (FlambdaWellKindedSpec.well_kinded_unit) for a table of slot kinds
      that gives the kind `Value` to each new slot not unboxed in turn.
      `changed_closure_rebuilt` asks the kind `Value` of what such a slot
      holds; the model's value slots may hold naked numbers (the
      compiler's have a kind, Value_slot.kind, which the model's
      representations do not carry). A premise on the unit instead (its
      well-kindedness, transferred through `rebuilt_value` to the second
      run) would need the allocation sites of `rebuilt_value` to be those
      of the representation, which the relation does not say.
    - The run of the unit does not go wrong (as for the third runs of
      FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v), so that `rr` relates the state of
      the second run to one of the third.
    - `check_dominated` and `changed_unstored`, which the closure-type

    - The statements are for the flag `Auto` and a least solution with no

  - Also this round:
    - `repr_reach_never_example_statement`
      (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresReach{Spec,Proofs}.v,

    - `related_static_values` and `related_initial`

  - Left (milestones (b) to (e) of the round, not started; what is in the
    way, and the route):
    - (b) wrappers of calls that return. The states within a wrapper of
      both runs bind the parameters of the wrapper, of different names in
      the two rebuilds, and are about to apply the continuation of the
      call: `related` cannot hold of them (`env_rel_c` asks a plain name
      to have one value in both runs). The relation needs a pending state,
      as `uc`'s `related_pending`, a disjunct of `related`. But
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v (a file of the types, not edited
      this round) destructs `related` as it is (`rr_values`) and builds it
      by its conjuncts (`rr_initial`), so `related` keeps its shape until
      that file uses `related_static_values` and `related_initial`. Then:
      `rr_call_wraps` (a call returning to a wrapper in both rebuilds,
      whose arguments, given the same results, give the continuation the
      same values), `cont_rel_wrap` (the two wrapper closures),
      `related_pending`, its case in `related_answered`, and in the
      construction `call_rebuilt` for `make_apply_wrapper` of the two
      rebuilds (the one wrapper name of each, its parameters the fresh
      names of each), with the wrapper metadata with types.
    - (c) over-applications at unknown arity: what is left of the
      over-application calls the result of the first code, whose code the
      flow of a variable does not give; `flow_ok` would carry a run
      invariant of the `Over_application` frames (as `uc`'s `REM`,
      `step_rem_ok`, and `rem_reach` of the Reach3 files), which
      `flow_run_total` (used by FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v) must
      still prove when the flow is any: an `REM` parameter of the flow
      changes the signature of `flow_run`.
    - (d) unboxed parameters: not in the way of the relation's shape (a
      parameter unboxed in the second rebuild is an unboxed name of `M`,
      whose fields `env_rel` relates); needs `rr_call` and `code_frame`
      with the shapes of the arguments and parameters (as `uc`'s), the
      entry of code with expanded parameters (`call_code_entered`), and
      the construction for `Unbox` decisions (`code_rebuilt`,
      `call_rebuilt`).
    - (e) changed closures held in fields and static sets that change: a
      plain name then holds, in the two runs, a block (or a closure) whose
      fields differ (the closure of the field in the new representation);
      `env_rel` would relate plain names by `rebuilt_changed`-like values
      rather than equality, a change of every case of the simulation.
    - The kind premise could go with a kind in the model's
      representations, or with a slot table in the unit's premises
      transferred to the rebuilds.
  - Nothing found false.
  - Files: new FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v (listed in rocq/Makefile after
    the Closures entries); changed

- The rebuilds on the machine of regions, after the plain rebuild: the
  premises of the refinement of the plain rebuild on the rebuilt unit
  discharged, and the rebuild with changes of calling convention
  (FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v extended, proved in
  FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.v; FlambdaReaperRegionsCcSpec.v, new, proved
  in FlambdaReaperRegionsCcProofs.v; lemmas in
  FlambdaReaperRegionsKeepsProofs.v, FlambdaReaperRegionsCcRebuildProofs.v
  and FlambdaReaperRegionsCcBaseProofs.v, new). Every statement holds;
  the fragment of the rebuild with changes of calling convention asks one
  more thing of the unit, that no code it binds returns a region. The
  unboxing rebuild is not reached.
  - What was in the way. `least_rebuild_regions_refines_statement` asked
    of the rebuilt unit `erasable` and the freshness of its symbols, and
    nothing said that the rebuild keeps them. The rebuild with changes of
    calling convention (`rebuild_expr_cc`) is a function of its own, and
    its refinement (FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs
    .rebuild_cc_with_refines_checked_local) is of the tables of the
    unit and of the first fresh name of the unit; on the erased unit the
    solution is the least solution of the unit with the constants used
    (`consts_used`), which is no table, and the first fresh name is that
    of the unit with its regions. Its tables are read off the graph and
    the traversal: the table of the code that cannot change
    (`derive_cannot_change`), the code deps (`unit_code_deps`), the
    metadata of the code (`bound_code_metadata`). Its wrappers take the
    results that the callee returns, at the kinds of the metadata of the
    callee, which `regions_plain` says nothing of.
  - The route.
    - What the rebuild keeps (FlambdaReaperRegionsKeepsProofs.v): a
      predicate of terms, `simples_ok P Q H` (every simple meets `P`,
      every symbol bound `Q`, the kinds of the parameters of every
      handler `H`), of which `expr_below`, `no_region_poison` and (with
      no handler with a parameter of the kind `Region`)
      `calls_return_no_region` are instances. `rebuild_expr_cc`, for any
      facts, solution, table, code deps and metadata, keeps it where `P`
      holds of every variable and every constant but the poison of the
      kind `Region`, and `H` of lists of no such kind and of what
      `filter_kept` keeps of them, on the terms of `calls_plain` (no
      argument of a call a variable of that kind, no result of a call
      and no parameter of a handler of that kind), which `regions_plain`
      implies, if the code that a call is known to call and that changes
      returns no region. A variable of the kind `Region` is always used,
      so `rewrite_simple` makes no poison of it; `poison_argument` gives
      the poison of the kind of an argument of a call; the wrappers have
      the kinds of the results. The plain rebuild is the instance where
      every code is in the table (FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs
      .rebuild_expr_cc_unchanged), where nothing changes.
    - The commutation (FlambdaReaperRegionsCcRebuildProofs.v): that of
      FlambdaReaperRegionsRebuildProofs.rebuild_erase_commutes_gen, case
      for case, with the unfoldings of `rebuild_expr_cc` and the lemmas
      of the parts reused; a call: the decision of the call
      (`apply_calling_convention_change`) is the same for both, as the
      decisions of code read the solution at the nodes of the code dep
      only, which are made nodes; the arguments kept, the call kind and
      the wrapper (`make_apply_wrapper_cc`) are the same, and the wrapper
      is erased to itself where the code returns no region; code: the
      erasure keeps the metadata of code (`bound_code_metadata_erase`)
      and erases the body only, under the same environment of the code
      (`cc_code_env`). The two traversals have the same accumulators up
      to the facts (`acc_rel` of FlambdaReaperRegionsTraverseProofs.v),
      so the same code deps, whose nodes are not the constants (`pinv`);
      `derive_cannot_change` reads the constructors and the accessors
      only, which are the same.
    - The refinement: FlambdaReaperRegionsCcBaseProofs
      .rebuild_cc_with_refines_gen is the proof of
      FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_cc_with_refines_checked_local
      with the solution given as propositions (closed, local reads used,
      field used exact) and any first fresh name that no continuation of
      the unit is (a generalisation of a lemma of a file of another
      stream, in a file of this one). The table of the code that cannot
      change of the unit with its regions is closed under the rules on
      the erased graph and `consts_used`, with the table of the witnesses
      and the constants (`cc_closed_consts`): no constructor is of the
      constants and no accessor reads into them (`positions_ok`), and a
      least solution has `usages` of the constants of the constants only
      (`least_usages_consts`, by induction on the derivations). The
      widgets read off a node keep their sources (`widgets_consts`), and
      each witness of the erased graph gives one code id
      (`traverse_witness_code_ids`).
  - New statements.
    - FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v: `rebuild_keeps_erasable_statement` (on
      `regions_plain`, for any facts and tables, the rebuilt unit is
      erasable wherever the unit is), `rebuild_keeps_fresh_statement`
      (and has no symbol at or above the base wherever the unit has
      none), `least_rebuild_regions_refines_checked_statement` (the
      refinement of `least_rebuild_regions_refines_statement`, which is
      kept, with the check of regions as the one premise on the rebuilt
      unit).
    - FlambdaReaperRegionsCcSpec.v: the fragment `regions_cc_plain`
      (`regions_plain` and `code_results_plain`: no result of the code
      that the unit binds of the kind `Region`);
      `rebuild_cc_erase_commutes_statement` (erasing the unit rebuilt
      with changes of calling convention is rebuilding the erased unit,
      for any tables and table, with the facts, code deps and metadata of
      the erased unit and the first fresh name of the unit);
      `cc_tables_erase_statement` (`derive_cannot_change` and
      `unit_code_deps` are those of the erased unit);
      `rebuild_cc_keeps_erasable_statement`,
      `rebuild_cc_keeps_fresh_statement`;
      `least_rebuild_cc_regions_refines_statement` (for a least solution
      of the graph of the unit with its regions, its least table, the
      premises of `least_rebuild_cc_refines_observably_statement` on the
      erased unit with `check_widgets_read_only` of the graph with
      regions, and the check of regions of the rebuilt unit, with any
      certificate: the rebuilt unit refines the unit on the machine of
      regions); `least_rebuild_cc_auto_regions_refines_statement` (with
      `Auto`, without the check of the graph);
      `regions_cc_example_statement` (a call in a region of code whose
      second parameter and two last results have no use: the code keeps
      one parameter and one result, the call one argument, the unused
      constant is removed, the rebuilt unit passes the check of regions
      with a certificate, and both exit with 7 on the machine of
      regions).
  - Premises added, and why. `code_results_plain` in the fragment of the
    rebuild with changes of calling convention: the parameters of a
    wrapper are the results that the callee returns, at the kinds of its
    metadata, and a result of the kind `Region` is always kept, so a
    wrapper with a parameter of that kind is not erased to itself (the
    erasure drops the parameter). `regions_plain` asks it of every call
    and not of code. None on the plain rebuild.
  - Left. The rebuild with unboxing (M3, not attempted): it names the
    variables of the unboxed fields after every name of the unit
    (`unit_unboxed_fields`, `fresh_name (names_of e)`), so the rebuild of
    the erased unit names them otherwise, and it reads the solution as
    tables (`unit_unboxed_fields F T`, `unit_changed_representations`):
    the refinement of the erased unit would need the proofs of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs.v over any first fresh name, and
    the solution with the constants used given as tables, with
    `derive_dominated` and `derive_to_unbox` of those tables related to
    those of the unit with its regions. Units with a parameter of the
    kind `Region`, a local set of several closures, or code that returns
    a region.
  - Nothing found false.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 852 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 10
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperRegionsCcSpec.v depend on no axiom outside the ledger

- Two copied proofs removed, the result types at the parameters of code
  at the nodes of the code dep, and the wider check of static blocks in
  the statements of FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v
  (FlambdaReaperTypes{Params,CallsUsed}{Spec,Proofs}.v, new;
  FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.v and FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesHeapProofs.v
  split; FlambdaReaperTypesCallsExitProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperTypesTraversalHeapExitProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.v and FlambdaReaperTypesLooseProofs.v
  changed; the headers of FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v and
  FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsSpec.v). The statements hold; the statements
  at the parameters take a test of the facts as a premise, which the
  traversal is expected to pass (left).
  - What was in the way. Two proofs were copies of the proofs of lemmas
    of other files up to their last step: `rebuild_unbox_ubh_heap`
    (FlambdaReaperTypesTraversalHeapExitProofs.v), of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesHeapProofs
    .rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild_heap, and the discharge of the
    hypotheses of the section `Run` in `cc_reach`
    (FlambdaReaperTypesCallsExitProofs.v), of
    FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_cc_with_refines_checked_local. The
    compiler rewrites the type of a parameter of code under the metadata
    of the variable of `code_dep.params` at its rank
    (unboxing_analysis.ml, types_rewriter.ml `rewrite_result_types`,
    `variable_pattern`), and the simulations relate the value of a
    parameter at its own node, which the traversal aliases to that node:
    the alias gives the parameter the sources of the node, and the node
    the usages of the parameter, so that in a solution that is only
    closed the parameter may have more sources and the node more usages,
    and the metadata of the node need not describe what the value of the
    parameter comes from. The `unit_premises` of
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v keep `unit_of_values`.
  - The route.
    - The splits, as `rebuild_repr_reach_modes_with_rr`: the old lemma
      keeps its name and type and is derived from the new one, the copy
      is deleted and its user calls the new lemma (about 170 and 160
      lines removed).
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesHeapProofs.rebuild_unbox_ubh_heap: the
      two rebuilt terms are related by `ubh` (the proof of
      `rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild_heap` up to its last step,
      moved); the old lemma is `ubh_refines` of it.
      FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_cc_with_related_checked_local:
      for the hypotheses of `cc_reach` (any table `code_deps` that agrees
      with the traversal where code changes, the widgets and the
      witnesses checked where code changes) and any premise `sets_exact`
      of the closures, `related` relates the first states whatever the
      allocations of the locations, and answers each step of the first
      run that allocates as they say; `located_refines` (new, the proof
      of `unit_refines` on any such relation) gives the refinement, so
      the old lemma is the instance where `code_deps` is that of the
      traversal and `sets_exact` is `False`; `cc_reach` is the instance
      where `sets_exact` is `True`, with `reach_of_located`.
    - The parameters. `param_node`: the node of rank `i` of `cd_params`
      of the code dep of the code (`Traverse.prepare_code` makes the
      variables of `code_dep.params` with `Variable.create`; the model
      makes a node for each). The check `params_aliased`, a test of the
      facts as `check_unknown_chains` is: for each parameter of the code
      of the unit and the node of its rank, no fact puts anything into
      the parameter but the alias from the node
      (FlambdaReaperIndirectDeadFramesSpec.only_alias_into) and no fact
      reads the node but that alias (`only_read_by`: no alias or
      propagation from it into another node, no alias on any source, no
      constructor, argument, accessor or use of it, no input any usage).
      A least solution of the rules is one of the rules with the fix of
      local fields (FlambdaReaperLeastProofs.least_local_of_least, under
      the premise of the rules before the fix), so inverting the
      derivations of those rules (FlambdaReaperTypesParamsProofs.v,
      section `Param`): the parameter has its sources, any source and
      source through the alias only, and the node its usages, any usage
      and use; so what the value of the parameter may come from may come
      to the node (`may_come_from_param`), and the metadata of the node
      describes it (FlambdaReaperTypesProofs.metadata_for_name_at): the
      value rebuilt at the node of the parameter is rebuilt under the
      metadata of the node of the code dep (`param_value_at_node`). The
      states are those of `cc_states_used`, and the type is rewritten
      at the node by FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsProofs
      .rewrite_result_type_at_sound_in.
    - The wider check. FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.related_facts reads
      the static environments by FlambdaReaperTypesUsedValuesProofs
      .statics_rel_rebuilt_used (the static blocks of the symbols with a
      use hold values, `unit_of_values_with_runs`); `cc_states_used`
      takes `used_values`, and `cc_states` (its old type) is its
      instance by `unit_of_values_with_instance`; `calls_states`,
      `calls_exit`, `repr_exit` and `cc_result_gen` take
      `used_premises`; each `_gen` of a statement is now the instance
      (tactic `used_instance`, by `unit_premises_used`) of a `_used_gen`
      that holds the proof. FlambdaReaperTypesLooseProofs.v gives those
      lemmas `unit_premises_used` of its premises.
  - New statements, all proved:
    - FlambdaReaperTypesParamsSpec.v (11): `param_value_at_node` (for a
      least solution, a unit that passes `params_aliased`, the parameter
      `p` of rank `i` of the code `id` whose node `r` of the code dep has
      a use: `p` has a use, and a value rebuilt at the node of `p` is
      rebuilt under the metadata of `r`);
      `param_type_end_to_end` (plain rebuild) and
      `cc_param_type_end_to_end` (rebuild with changes of calling
      convention), each with `_landed`, `_local`, `_local_landed`: at
      each state that the run of the unit comes to and steps from, where
      `p` has the value `v`, the type that the result types give
      `type_var`, which holds of `v`, rewritten under the metadata of
      `r` (`rewrite_result_type_at`), holds of the value of `p` at a
      state that the rebuilt run comes to. `param_example` (and
      `_landed`, computed): the unit of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesSpec.CallExample, whose code 50 reads
      the field 1 of its parameter `x40`; its node is the made node 6,
      whose only source is the block `x10` of 7 and 8; the unit passes

      1 is 8; rewritten under the metadata of the node (a single source,

      poison and 8 at the `Let` of `x43` (5 steps), of which it holds.
    - FlambdaReaperTypesCallsUsedSpec.v (25):
      `used_premises_instance` (`unit_premises` gives `used_premises`,
      which asks `used_values` in place of `unit_of_values`), and each
      statement of FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v but the example with
      `used_premises`, each with `_landed`, `_local`, `_local_landed`:
      `unbox_calls_types_end_to_end_used`,
      `repr_calls_types_end_to_end_used`,
      `result_type_end_to_end_used`, `cc_result_type_end_to_end_used`,
      `unbox_calls_result_type_end_to_end_used`,
      `calls_unboxed_result_type_end_to_end_used` (the `_local_landed`

      `unbox_calls_result_end_to_end_used_local_landed`,
      `calls_unboxed_result_end_to_end_used_local_landed`). The old
      statements are proved as their instances.
  - Premises added, and why. For the parameters: `params_aliased`, a
    test of the facts (the traversal's facts are not yet known to pass
    it in general, see Left); that the node of the parameter has a use
    (the rebuild makes poison of a parameter with no use, the change of
    calling conventions deletes it, and the compiler drops its type,
    `Delete` in `rewrite_result_types`); and the premises of
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsUsedSpec.v (`used_premises`, weaker than
    `unit_premises`), with the checks of the table for the rebuild with
    changes of calling convention. For the wider check, none but the
    check itself, which reads the solution.
  - Existing names and types are unchanged. Internal lemmas of
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.v changed type: `related_facts` takes
    `statics_of_used_values` of the symbols with a use, and
    `calls_states`, `calls_exit`, `repr_exit`, `cc_result_gen` take
    `used_premises`; `cc_states` keeps its type.
  - Left:
    - That the traversal of every unit whose variables are bound once
      passes `params_aliased`: it puts into a parameter of code only the

    - The parameters in the fragment `loose`, and the parameters that

      .calls_unboxed_result_type_end_to_end_statement).
    - The wider check in the statements of FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsSpec.v
      and FlambdaReaperTypesLooseSpec.v.
  - Nothing found false.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 888 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 36
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperTypesParamsSpec.v (11),
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsUsedSpec.v (25), and those of
    FlambdaReaperTypesCallsSpec.v, FlambdaReaperTypesLooseSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesHeapSpec.v and FlambdaReaperRebuildSpec.v,
    whose proofs changed, depend on no axiom outside the ledger
