- The local unboxing on the machine of regions, and the check of regions
  under the plain rebuild (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.v, new, proved in
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxProofs.v with the lemmas of
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxBaseProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxRebuildProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxTraverseProofs.v and
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxKeepsProofs.v, new; FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v
  extended, proved in FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.v). The rebuild with
  unboxing of `rebuild_unit_unbox` (the mode of the branch, no change of
  representation: that of the local statements of
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesSpec.v) commutes with the erasure, for any
  tables, on units with no closure, call or code, and refines the unit on
  the machine of regions for the least solution of the graph with regions
  and the tables of the rules, with the premises of the least local
  statement on the erased unit and the check of regions of the rebuilt
  unit. The check of regions is not kept by the plain rebuild (a
  counterexample, the known artefact of the boxed poison). The unboxing
  across calls is not reached.
  - What was in the way. The rebuild with unboxing names the variables of
    the unboxed fields after every name of the unit (`unit_unboxed_fields`,
    `fresh_name (names_of e)`); the erased unit lacks the variables of the
    regions, so its own rebuild may name them from a smaller first name.
    The local refinement (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs
    .rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild) is of the unit's own first fresh
    name, and of tables closed under the rules on the graph of the unit
    it rebuilds; the least solution of the graph with regions is not
    closed on the erased graph (it has not the alias of the variable of an
    end to the constants), and the solution that is (FlambdaReaperRegions
    Proofs.consts_used) is propositions, where the unboxing reads tables.
    The fragment `local_unboxing` names the fields after the erased unit's
    names. With any tables, an allocation to unbox may dominate the
    variable of a region, and the rebuild then makes the begin `Invalid`;
    and a field of the kind `Region` would make the rebuild give a handler
    a parameter of that kind, which the erasure drops.
  - The route.
    - The local refinement for any first name `n0` above every name of the
      unit, and a solution `sol` apart from the tables `T` that the unboxed
      fields are read off (`coherent T sol`: the same of the variables, of
      the sources and of the usages, and every usage that `T` has)
      (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxBaseProofs.rebuild_unbox_refines_rebuild_sol):
      the proof of `rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild` with those left
      free, from its lemmas (`rebuild_ub`, `ub_refines`, the provenance of
      the fields), in a file of this stream; FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefines
      Proofs.v is not edited.
    - The fragment of the local unboxing reads the solution of the
      variables only (`local_expr_ext`), and of the unboxed fields their
      shapes only (`ushape`, `local_expr_shapes`), which do not depend on
      the first name (`unboxed_fields_of_names_shapes`, through the depth
      of FlambdaReaperUnboxingFuelProofs.name_unboxed_fields_depth).
    - The commutation (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxRebuildProofs
      .rebuild_u_erase_commutes): that of FlambdaReaperRegionsRebuild
      Proofs.rebuild_erase_commutes_gen, case for case, the environments
      of the rebuild with unboxing related by its `env_rel`; the `Let`s of
      simples that the unboxing makes (`bind_fields`, `bind_block_fields`,
      `load_unboxed_field`) commute with the erasure; the
      `Local_allocation` of an unboxed block is dropped by the rebuild with
      regions and by the erasure alike; the variable of a begin is not
      unboxed (`begins_kept`); the decisions of a handler are the same in
      both and make no parameter of the kind `Region` (`decision_plain`)
      where no field of an unboxing is of that kind; the two solutions
      differ by the usages of the constants, which the rebuild reads
      nowhere.
    - The graph (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxTraverseProofs.v): the accessors
      of a unit with no closure, call or code read no field of the kind
      `Region` (`closure_free_reads_plain`, the traversal of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs.closure_free_reads_blocks with the
      kinds), so no field of an unboxing is of that kind, at any depth
      (`ukinds_ok`, `mk_unboxed_fields_kinds`); the unit with regions is
      of the fragment of the flow wherever its erasure is
      (`in_flow_fragment_of_erase`), and its edges give the variable of
      each begin any source (`begins_any_source`): in closed tables it
      has any source, and `derive_dominated` dominates no such node.
    - What the rebuild keeps (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxKeepsProofs.v):
      `rebuild_expr_u` keeps `simples_ok P Q H` of FlambdaReaperRegions
      KeepsProofs.v on the fragment, the poison of a field of an unboxed
      block being of the kind of the field; `erasable` and the freshness
      of the symbols follow.
    - The refinement: FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.regions_refine_of_erased;
      the commutation with `consts_used` of the least solution for the
      erased unit; its closedness and exactness on the erased graph
      (`consts_used_closed`, `consts_used_exact`); the plain refinement of
      FlambdaReaperRegionsBaseProofs.rebuild_refines_observably_gen; the
      local refinement above with `n0` the first fresh name of the unit
      with regions (`fresh_name_erase_le`: the erasure adds no name);
      `check_unboxed_reads` from FlambdaReaperLeastProofs
      .least_unboxed_reads on the graph with regions, whose accessors and
      constructors are those of the erased graph.
  - New statements.
    - FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.v: the fragment `regions_local`
      (`regions_plain` and `closure_free`) and `regions_not_unboxed` (no
      allocation to unbox dominates the variable of a begin,
      `region_binders`); `unboxed_fields_erase_statement` (the unboxed
      fields of the unit and of the erased unit are the same, from any
      first name); `to_unbox_erase_statement` (`derive_to_unbox`, for any
      table of the code that cannot change, and `least_to_unbox` are those
      of the erased unit; `derive_dominated` reads the solution alone);
      `local_unboxing_names_statement` (`local_unboxing` holds with the
      fields named from any first name);
      `regions_not_unboxed_closed_statement` (closed tables of the graph
      with regions and `derive_dominated` give `regions_not_unboxed`);
      `rebuild_unbox_erase_commutes_statement` (on the fragment, with
      `regions_not_unboxed`, for any tables: erasing the unit rebuilt
      with unboxing is rebuilding the erased unit with its facts and the
      fields named after every name of the unit with regions);
      `rebuild_unbox_keeps_erasable_statement`,
      `rebuild_unbox_keeps_fresh_statement`;
      `least_rebuild_unbox_regions_refines_statement` (for a least
      solution of the graph with regions, `derive_dominated`,
      `least_to_unbox` of that graph, the premises of
      FlambdaReaperLeastSpec.least_rebuild_unbox_refines_observably
      _statement on the erased unit with the check of the reads on the
      graph with regions, and the check of regions of the rebuilt unit
      with any certificate: the rebuilt unit refines the unit on the
      machine of regions); `regions_unbox_example_statement` (a region
      with a local block of two fields, the first read: the rules unbox
      it, the rebuilt unit binds the variable 14 of the field to 7, reads
      it in place of the load, drops the block and its
      `Local_allocation`, passes the check of regions, and both exit with
      7 on the machine of regions; the least solution is not closed on the
      erased graph).
    - FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v: `region_check_not_kept_statement` (M3,
      below).
  - Premises added, and why. `regions_not_unboxed` in the commutation
    with any tables (an allocation to unbox that dominates the variable of
    a begin makes the begin `Invalid` in the rebuild with regions, and
    the erased unit has no such `Let`); the refinement does not ask it,
    as the tables of the rules never dominate such a variable. The check
    of the reads (`check_reads_read_only`) is asked of the graph with
    regions, not of the erased graph: it reads the aliases, which the
    erasure changes (an alias of the variable of each end to the
    constants); the tables of the rules are those of the graph with
    regions, which the compiler computes (`to_unbox_erase_statement`:
    the same). Left out of the premises of the least statement: no local
    field or no projection (the fragment has no projection).
  - Found false (M3): the check of regions does not follow for the plain
    rebuild. `region_check_not_kept_statement`: the unit `boxed` of
    FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v (a local boxed number whose contents
    nothing reads) passes the check of regions with `cert`, and its plain
    rebuild for the least solution fails it with every certificate: the
    rebuild makes the boxed number poison and keeps its
    `Local_allocation`, which the check refuses after what is not an
    allocation. So no certificate for the rebuilt unit can be built from
    one for the unit there. An artefact of the model: rebuild.ml replaces
    the whole `Box_number`, mode included, by poison. The rebuild with
    unboxing does the same with a local boxed number that it does not
    unbox. Elsewhere a certificate for the rebuilt unit would be that of
    the unit with the lifetimes of the parameters of each handler
    filtered by its mask (poison lives for ever, and a dead `Let` goes
    with its `Local_allocation`); not proved.
  - Left. The unboxing across calls (M2, not attempted): the commutation
    of `rebuild_expr_u` in the mode of the fix with calls, code, wrappers
    and closures; the statements of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCalls
    Spec.v ask the tables to be closed on the graph of the erased unit,
    which the least solution of the graph with regions is not in any unit
    that ends a region (shown of the example), so they would apply as
    they are to other tables (with the tuples of `consts_used`), whose
    unboxed fields and decisions are not related here to those of the
    least solution, or their proofs would need the solution apart from
    the tables as done here for the local unboxing; and they name the
    fields and the wrappers after the names of the erased unit. The
    positive part of M3 (a certificate built away from the boxed
    poison). Units with a parameter of the kind `Region`, a local set of
    several closures, or code that returns a region.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 898 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 10
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v depend on no axiom outside the ledger
    (own-assum.sh: 18 statements, 18 closed under the global context).
- Calls whose code is not known that never return and complete a partial
  application: the first shape of
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverShapesSpec.v admitted
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2Types{Spec,Proofs}.v, new; the
  simulation `uc` of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSimProofs.v and its
  construction in FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs.v extended in
  place; FlambdaReaperPartialGroupsProofs.v and the NeverShapes files
  changed). The second shape (an over-application whose remainder never
  returns) is not admitted: what is in the way is below. Nothing found
  false.
  - What was in the way.
    - `never_targets` asks the code that the flow brings to the callee `f`
      of a call of unknown arity that never returns to have no more
      parameters than the call has groups, so that the call is a full call
      or an over-application, never a partial application, which the first
      run would give to the frame that the second run drops. A call that
      completes a partial application gives fewer groups: the partial
      application holds the others. The bound on those is a property of
      the run of the first unit, and the relation's run invariants
      (`pa_inv`, `flow_ok`, `step_over_ok`, `step_rem_ok`) are fixed by the
      types of `related2_answered` and `uc_refines_flow`, which the files
      of the types (FlambdaReaperTypesCallsExitProofs.v,
      FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.v) and of the changes of
      representation use with their types: a new invariant would be a new
      parameter of `uc`, `related2` and `flow_run`.
    - The one bit that the existing invariants give: `flow_ok` puts the
      code of a closure that `f` holds in `RCH true f` and that of the
      closure of what `f` holds (a partial application included) in
      `RCH false f`. Where the code is in `RCH false f` and not in
      `RCH true f`, `f` holds partial applications of it only; and a
      partial application that the machine makes holds a group of
      arguments at least (`partial_or_over_application` asks
      `0 < provided`), which `pa_inv` did not record.
    - The construction (`rebuild_uc_h` and the lemmas of code and
      bindings) was stated of `calls_expr_n` of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverSpec.v, whose check of a call is
      fixed.
  - The route.
    - `pa_ok` (FlambdaReaperPartialGroupsProofs.v) asks of each partial
      application `pa_made`: `pa_bound`, unchanged (the closures files
      build it from the flow's own invariant), and a group of arguments at
      least (`args_arity <> []`, from the test of `shape_of_code_call`:
      `partial_groups_nonempty`; the hypothesis of the call lemmas,
      `P_partial_made`, `step_values_bound`, gives it). `pa_ok_partial`
      has it as a last conjunct, so that the uses of its first are
      unchanged. `pa_inv`, `pa_inv_step`, `pa_inv_initial`, `pa_inv_run`
      keep their names and types; `pa_inv` is stronger.
    - `never_targets2`, `never_rel2` and the case `uc_call_ind_never2`
      (last of the cases of `uc`): for a call of unknown arity, code with as
      many parameters as the call has groups plus one, where `RCH true f id`
      fails. Its step (`never_call_answered2_hp`, called from
      `main_answered`) reads the bound off `pa_inv`
      (`pa_inv_partial_groups`): the closure of the code in `f` itself
      would put it in `RCH true f`; a partial application holds a group,
      so that the call gives the code all its parameters, and
      `never_closure_answered_hp` applies. `uc_agree` and `uc_never_ext`
      (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs.v) have the new case.
    - The construction made generic in the check of a call (Section
      `Generic` of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs.v: `callok`,
      with `callok_rebuilt`, that the two rebuilds relate a call that it
      accepts): `rebuild_ok_expr_hw`, `code_rebuilt_nc_hw`,
      `code_rebuilt_hw`, `bindings_rebuilt_hw`, `rebuild_uc_hw` hold of the
      terms of `calls_expr_w` of it (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2Spec
      .v: `calls_expr_n` with the check of a call given). `calls_expr_n` is
      `calls_expr_w` of `call_ok_n` by conversion (`calls_n_w`, by
      `reflexivity`: the two fixpoints are the same but for the check of a
      call), so that `rebuild_ok_expr_h`, `code_rebuilt_nc_h`,
      `code_rebuilt_h`, `bindings_rebuilt_h`, `rebuild_uc_h` and their
      instances at `False` keep their names and types, proved from the
      generic lemmas with `call_rebuilt`. For the new fragment:
      `never_code_shape2`, `uc_result_ind_never2`, `call_rebuilt_never2`
      (copies of the lemmas of `uc_call_ind_never` with
      `uc_call_ind_never2`), `call_rebuilt2`, `rebuild_uc_n2`, and, beside
      the top-level lemmas of the never fragment (unedited),
      `rebuild_unbox_modes_with_refines_cc_never2_gen` and
      `rebuild_unbox_modes_refines_cc_never2_gen`.
    - The check (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2Spec.v):
      `never_code_ok2` is `never_code_ok`, or code of `nr` that keeps its
      calling convention, with one parameter more than the call has groups
      at most, and `reach true f id` false (`reach true` is `flow_reach`,
      the closures that may come to `f`, of which a partial application is
      none); `ind_never_ok2`, `ind_call_ok_n2`, `call_ok_n2`;
      `calls_unboxing_reach_never2_nr`, `calls_unboxing_never2_nr` (read
      off the first rebuilt unit), `calls_unboxing_unit_never2_nr` (read
      off the unit's own analysis). The checks are monotone in the check of
      a call (`calls_w_mono`), and in the reach (`call_mono_n2`):
      `never_code_ok2` reads `reach true` negated, so a smaller reach makes
      it weaker, as the other checks; so `used_reach_covered` gives the
      unit's form (`unit_never2_covered`).
  - New statements, all proved:
    - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2Spec.v (8):
      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_refines_cc`,
      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_refines_observably`,
      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_refines_observably_local` (the statements of

      `calls_unboxing_never2_nr`), `rebuild_unbox_unit_never2_refines_cc`,

    - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNever2TypesSpec.v (18): with types

      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_with_types_{refines_cc,local,observably}`,

      with the primitives of the heap (those of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsHeapSpec.v)
      `rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_{cc_heap,heap_local,up_to_heap}`,

    - `never_partial2_example` (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverShapesSpec
      .v, computed): the unit of `never_partial_example` is of
      `calls_unboxing_never2_nr` and of `calls_unboxing_unit_never2_nr`,
      with the least solutions and the code of `used_never_returns` (50 and
      51); `flow_reach` of the first rebuilt unit brings neither 50 nor 51
      to `x32`, which holds the partial application; the rebuilt unit
      refines its first rebuild (`rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_refines_cc`) and
      the unit up to observation
      (`rebuild_unbox_nr_ind2_refines_observably`), not by computing the

      fragment of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesNeverSpec.v refuses the
      unit.
  - Premises added, and why: none to existing statements. The new
    statements have the premises of those they copy, with the new
    fragments in place of the old.
  - Changed in meaning (names and types unchanged): `pa_ok`, so `pa_inv`,
    is stronger (a partial application holds a group); the
    inductive `uc` has one more case, `uc_call_ind_never2`, after
    `uc_let_heap` (the scheme `uc_mut` has one more case after that of
    `uc_let_heap`). A proof that destructs `uc` or `pa_ok` needs the new
    case or conjunct. One type changed: that of
    FlambdaReaperPartialGroupsProofs.pa_ok_partial (an iff) has the
    conjunct `args_arity <> []` at its end, as its old converse no longer
    holds; its uses read its first conjuncts and are unchanged. The lemmas
    of the construction for `calls_expr_n` are now instances of generic
    ones, with their names and types.
  - Left:
    - The bound read off the chain of witnesses. The flow puts a partial
      application of `k` groups at the allocation of the first result of
      the `k`-th witness of unknown arity of its code
      (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesReach2Proofs.partial_reach: `chain_reach`
      gives the source `y'` of `f` whose call witness is the `k`-th of the
      code dep), so the number of witnesses that follow `y'`'s in the chain
      bounds the parameters left; a check `chain_longer` over the
      constructor facts of the first rebuilt unit (`W --Normal 0--> v
      --call witness of unknown arity--> W'`) would admit calls that
      complete partial applications of any depth. The simulation needs it
      as a run invariant beside `flow_ok`, so a parameter of `uc`,
      `related2` and `flow_run`, whose types the other files use; the bound
      of one group is what `RCH true`/`RCH false` and `pa_inv` give
      without it.
    - The over-application whose remainder never returns
      (`never_over_example`). It needs a case of `cont_rel` for an
      `Over_application` whose two continuations are not related, at the
      kshape of the results of the code over-applied (a handler, `true`,
      which `code_K` gives the return continuation of that code). Every
      lemma that reads a `cont_rel` at a handler kshape then has to show
      that the first run's frame does not exit: `cont_rel_steps_hp`
      (`Exit` is `False`) holds of the new case only if what is left of
      the over-application makes no partial application, which only the
      run (`cont_rem_ok` of `step_rem_ok`) says. So `cont_rel_steps_hp`,
      `run_call_no_exit_hp` and their callers would take `cont_rem_ok`, a
      change of their types; and the check would ask every code of `REM`
      (`rem_reach`: what is left of any over-application may call) to
      never return, keep its calling convention and be completed by the
      groups left, as `step_rem_ok` ties what is left to `REM` and not to
      the code over-applied: too coarse for units with another
      over-application that returns. A run invariant tying what is left of
      each over-application to the code over-applied (the flow has it: the
      frame's node is the first result of a witness of that code) is again
      a parameter of the relation.
    - The forms where `nr` is derived: the unit forms here
      (`rebuild_unbox_unit_never2_*`, with types and with the heap) take
      `used_never_returns` of the unit's analysis; the others take `nr`
      given, of which `used_never_returns` is an instance, with no
      statement of its own.

      fact that reads a parameter of a code dep (or its witness of known
      arity) is the alias of `traverse_code` into the parameter of that
      rank of the code (`pi_reads`); a fact that puts something into a
      variable puts it into a variable of a `Let` or of `hparams` (the

- The relation of the changes of representation read only through its
  lemmas; calls of code that changes its calling convention that return
  through a wrapper of each rebuild; and calls of unknown arity that
  over-apply (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresWraps{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresOver{Spec,Proofs}.v, new; the
  relation of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresSimProofs.v, the
  construction of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresReachProofs.v changed). No
  counterexample: the statements hold; the existing statements are
  unchanged in name, type and meaning. Of the milestones of the round
  (the relation read through lemmas; wrappers of calls that return;
  over-applications at unknown arity; unboxed parameters; changed closures
  in fields and static sets), the first three are done; the last two are
  not started (see Left).
  - What was in the way.
    - FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v destructed `related` (`rr_values`)
      and built it by its conjuncts (`rr_initial`), and
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v read its environments and its
      static bindings by its shape (`repr_runs`, `step_blocks`), so that
      `related` could have no other kind of state.
    - Within a wrapper of a call, the two runs bind the parameters of their
      wrappers, which have other names in the two rebuilds (the first names
      them from `fresh_name (names_of e)`, the second after the variables
      of the unboxed fields, from the same start): `env_rel_c`, which asks
      a plain name to have one value in both runs, cannot hold there. In
      the example below, the parameter 61 of the first rebuild's wrapper
      is, in the second rebuild, a variable of an unboxed field.
    - `repr_runs` (the closure types end to end) reads, at a state of the
      first run that a state of the unit's run is related to, the value of
      a variable of the unit in the second run: within a wrapper, the
      relation has to say which names the wrappers bind, and that no
      variable of the unit is one of them.
    - Between the `Let_cont` of the wrapper and the entry into the callee,
      the two runs are at calls returning to continuations of other names
      (the two wrappers), which `conts_rel`, a relation of the frames at
      the same names, does not relate.
    - What is left of an over-application calls the value that the first
      code returns, whose code the flow of a variable does not give; and
      `flow_run_total`, which FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v uses,
      must still hold where the flow is any.
  - The route.
    - Interface lemmas (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresSimProofs.v,
      last section): `related_static_values`, `related_initial`,
      `related_env` (the environments related at the names that are not
      those of the wrappers, `env_rel_cw`) and `related_let_static` (at a
      `Let_static`, the static bindings related by `rr_bindings`).
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v reads the relation only through
      `related_static_values`, `related_initial` and `related_answered`,
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v only through those and
      `related_env`, `related_let_static`; no other file destructs it
      (checked with grep).
    - Wrappers. The relation has a parameter `W`, the names that the
      rebuilds give the parameters of wrappers (a section variable after
      `RCH`). `related` is `related_main` (the old relation),
      `related_call` (both at a call of `rr_call` returning to the wrapper
      of each, `call_ok_c`, the environments by `env_rel_c`, the two
      wrappers' frames related) or `related_pending` (both within their
      wrapper, applying the same continuation, related by `cont_rel`, to
      the same values, the environments related outside `W`). New case of
      `rr`, `rr_call_wraps`: the `Let_cont` of a wrapper and a call of
      `rr_call` returning to it, in each run, of parameters of `W`, the
      two wrappers meeting `wrap_ok` (the same kinds, what the second
      states holding where what the first states does, and, bound to the
      same results, the same arguments for the continuation). New case of
      `cont_rel`, `cont_rel_wrap` (the two wrapper closures, made at
      states related by `env_rel_c`, the continuation related). The
      simulation (`main_answered`, `call_answered`, `pending_answered`,
      `related_answered`): the `Let_cont` of the two wrappers is answered
      by one step, to `related_call`; the call enters related code, the
      wrappers as its return frames (`call_entered`); a return to them
      (`cont_answered`) comes to `related_pending`; its `Apply_cont` to
      the continuation of the call (`cont_answered` of the continuation).
    - Over-applications. The relation has a parameter `REM`, the code that
      what is left of an over-application may call; `flow_run` (of the
      first run) is now `flow_ok` and
      FlambdaReaperPartialGroupsProofs.step_rem_ok of `REM` at each state;
      `flow_run_total` asks `REM` to hold of all code, as `RCH` of all,
      and still holds. New case of `rr`, `rr_call_over`: a call of
      unknown arity of a plain variable `f`, any number of groups of
      arguments, the code of `RCH false f` and of `REM` having a plain
      `my_closure`. New case of `cont_rel`, `cont_rel_over`: what is left
      of an over-application in both runs, the code of `REM` plain.
      `closure_shape_rel` now gives the shape of an over-application of
      the second run (the same, of related code); `closure_call_answered`
      answers a call of a closure of any shape (an over-application
      entering the code with `Over_application` frames related by
      `cont_rel_over`); `cont_answered` takes `cont_rem_ok` of the first
      run and answers an `Over_application` frame as such a call;
      `over_call_step` answers `rr_call_over`.
    - The two parameters in the files of the types:
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprExitProofs.v quantifies `W` (`rr_any`) and
      takes `REM` to be all code; its lemmas keep their parameters, so
      FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.v is unchanged.
      FlambdaReaperTypesReprClosuresProofs.v keeps `W` explicit
      (`rr_blocks`) and reads the variables of the unit outside it.
    - The construction (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresProofs.v):
      `Wf`, the names from `ff` or from `ffu`, and `REMf` of `rem`; the
      relation of the two rebuilds is now built for the widest fragment
      (`rebuild_rr` with `repr_expr_o`, `call_rebuilt_o`), its `W`
      excluding the variables of the unit
      (`rebuild_repr_over_modes_with_rr_names`). A call through wrappers

      `args_are_params`), equal by
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs.WithoutUnboxing

      (`r_within_x`, `x_within_o`, `repr_reach_within_wraps_unit`,
      `repr_wraps_within_over_unit`), and the lemmas of the narrower
      fragments (`rebuild_repr_reach_modes_with_rr_names`, `_with_rr`,
      `_with_refines_cc`, and their `wraps` forms) keep their names as

    - The flow (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresOverProofs.v,
      `first_flow_o`): the run of the first rebuilt unit is as
      `flow_reach2` and `rem_reach` say, the latter by

    - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresWrapsSpec.v:

    - `repr_over_example_statement` (`OverExample`, computed): `x61`, the

  - Premises added, and why.
    - `kinds_kept` of the decisions of the results and of those of the
      continuation (a check of the fragment): where the continuation keeps
      a result that the callee does not return, the first rebuild gives
      it poison of the kind of the result in the call's return arity, the
      second of the kind of the continuation's decision; the wrapper of
      the first takes the kinds of its parameters from the decisions of
      the code, the second's from the return arity. The compiler's
      decisions take their kinds from the same arity.
    - Calls through wrappers are of code that changes its calling
      convention only: the wrapper of a call of code that keeps it is made
      by FlambdaReaperRebuild.make_apply_wrapper in the first rebuild,
      which states, with types, what the continuation keeps of its
      parameters, and in the second with `cont_kept_subkinds`; that case is
      left.
    - Calls that may over-apply return (`apply_never_returns` false) to a
      continuation that keeps every result, as the calls whose code is not
      known of the last fragment do.
  - Left (milestones (d) and (e), not started):

  - Nothing found false.

    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresWrapsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresOverSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesClosuresCallsSpec.v,

- The three generalized copies of the regions files removed, and the
  rebuild with unboxing in the mode of the fix on the machine of regions,
  for the units of the local unboxing (FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs.v generalized in place;
  FlambdaReaperRegionsBaseProofs.v and FlambdaReaperRegionsCcBaseProofs.v
  deleted; FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxBaseProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxRebuildProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxKeepsProofs.v, FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsCcProofs.v and FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxProofs.v
  changed, and the headers of FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v,
  FlambdaReaperRegionsCcSpec.v and FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.v;
  FlambdaReaperRegionsFixedSpec.v, new, proved in
  FlambdaReaperRegionsFixedProofs.v, new). The statements of the unboxing
  across calls (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSpec.v) are applied, as
  they are, to the erased unit, with the tables of the graph with regions
  completed by the aliases of the variables with any source to the
  constants; the commutation with the erasure is proved for the mode of
  the fix on the units of the local unboxing only. Nothing found false.
  - What was in the way.
    - M1: rg1 to rg3 needed the base refinements for any first fresh name
      and a solution given as propositions, and each put a generalized
      copy of the base proof in a file of its own:
      `rebuild_with_refines_gen` (a copy of
      FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_with_refines_checked_local),
      `rebuild_cc_with_refines_gen` (of
      rebuild_cc_with_refines_checked_local; the split of ty3,
      `rebuild_cc_with_related_checked_local`, still read the tables and
      the unit's own first fresh name, so it was not general enough) and
      `rebuild_unbox_refines_rebuild_sol` (of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs.rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild).
    - M2: the statements of the unboxing across calls ask the tables to
      pass `check_closed` on the graph of the unit they rebuild; the
      erased graph has an alias of the variable of each end to the
      constants (the erasure binds it to the unit value), which the least
      solution of the graph with regions has not
      (`regions_unbox_example_statement`). Those statements are of the
      tables, so the solution with the constants used of rg1
      (`consts_used`, propositions) does not apply to them. They name the
      variables of the unboxed fields (and the wrappers) after every name
      of the unit they rebuild, which for the erased unit lacks the
      variables of the begins. And the commutation of rg3 was of the mode
      of the branch only.
  - The route.
    - M1, in place, each old lemma kept with its name and type as an
      instance (the copies' proofs moved, not copied):
      - FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_with_refines_gen (any solution
        closed under the rules with the fix of local fields and the
        post-processing exact, as propositions, and any first fresh name
        `ff` that no continuation of the unit is);
        `rebuild_with_refines_checked_local` is its instance at
        `solution_of_tables T` and `fresh_name (names_of e)`, with the
        soundness of the three checks and `fresh_name_not_continuation`
        (new, the step that the two proofs had inline).
      - FlambdaReaperRebuildProofs.rebuild_cc_with_related_gen: the
        simulation of `rebuild_cc_with_related_checked_local` with the
        solution and the first fresh name left free (the relation
        `related` takes `ff`); the old lemma is its instance as above.
        The refinement on the regions side is
        `located_refines` of it (FlambdaReaperRegionsCcProofs
        .rebuild_cc_refines_observably_gen, moved there).
      - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesProofs: `coherent` (moved, a solution
        apart from the tables, the same of the variables, sources and
        usages), `coherent_tables`, and
        `rebuild_unbox_refines_rebuild_sol` (any first name `n0` of the
        fields above every name of the unit, any `ff` of the plain
        rebuild, a `coherent` solution); `rebuild_unbox_with_refines_rebuild`
        is its instance with `n0 = ff = fresh_name (names_of e)`.
      - The two wrappers up to observation that the base files had,
        `rebuild_refines_observably_gen` and
        `rebuild_cc_refines_observably_gen`, are in
        FlambdaReaperRegionsProofs.v and FlambdaReaperRegionsCcProofs.v.
        FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxBaseProofs.v keeps its lemmas that are no
        copies (the fragment read off the variables and the shapes of the
        fields); its header says so.
    - M2, the tables. `alias_consts T`: `T` with one alias of each
      variable with any source to the constants (the aliases of
      `consts_used`), nothing else. The rebuilds read no alias and the
      unboxed fields are read off the other tables, so every rebuild with
      unboxing, in either mode, of any unit, with `alias_consts T` is the
      one with `T` (`alias_consts_rebuild_statement`, by conversion). The
      statement of the calls up to observation
      (`rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_observably_local_statement`) is then
      applied to the erased unit and `alias_consts T`, its premises asked
      there: the rebuilt erased unit is the rebuild of the erased unit
      with `T`.
    - M2, the names. Where the largest name of the unit is one that the
      erasure keeps (`fresh_name` of the unit and of the erased unit the
      same), the fields of the erased unit are those of the unit
      (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.unboxed_fields_erase_statement), so
      the rebuild that the calls statement refines is the one that the
      commutation gives.
    - M2, the commutation. The commutation of rg3
      (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxRebuildProofs.rebuild_u_erase_commutes)
      and what the rebuild keeps (FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxKeepsProofs
      .rebuild_u_simples) are now of either mode (the only difference on
      units with no call or code: a parameter of a handler kept with no
      use is `Keep_unused`, of its kind, never `Region`); the old lemmas
      of the units are instances at the mode of the branch
      (`rebuild_unbox_mode_erase_commutes_with`,
      `rebuild_unbox_mode_keeps_simples`, `_erasable`, `_fresh`).
  - New statements (FlambdaReaperRegionsFixedSpec.v, all proved):
    - `alias_consts_rebuild_statement` (above).
    - `rebuild_unbox_fixed_erase_commutes_statement`: on
      `regions_local` with `regions_not_unboxed`, for any tables, erasing
      the unit rebuilt in the mode of the fix is rebuilding the erased
      unit in that mode, with its facts and the fields named after every
      name of the unit.
    - `rebuild_unbox_fixed_keeps_erasable_statement`,
      `rebuild_unbox_fixed_keeps_fresh_statement`.
    - `rebuild_unbox_fixed_regions_refines_statement`: on the same
      fragment, where the largest name of the unit is kept by the
      erasure, for any tables `T`, table of the code that cannot change
      and tables of the unboxing, with the premises of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsSpec
      .rebuild_unbox_fixed_refines_observably_local_statement on the
      erased unit and `alias_consts T`, and the check of regions of the
      rebuilt unit: the unit rebuilt in the mode of the fix with `T`

    - `least_rebuild_unbox_fixed_regions_refines_statement`: the same for
      the least solution of the graph with regions, `derive_dominated`,
      `least_to_unbox` and `derive_cannot_change` of that graph, where

    - `regions_fixed_example_statement`: `local_pair` of rg3: its least

      unit is of `calls_unboxing` with the completion; the rebuild in the
      mode of the fix is that of the branch; and, through the least
      statement, the rebuilt unit refines the unit on the machine of
      regions.
  - Premises added, and why.
    - `fresh_name (names_of (erase [] e)) = fresh_name (names_of e)`: the
      calls statements name the fields after the names of the erased
      unit; otherwise the two rebuilt units differ by the names of those
      variables only (alpha-equivalent; FlambdaAlphaSpec
      .alpha_equivalent_sound_statement would carry the refinement, but
      that the rebuild with unboxing is equivariant in the names of the
      fields is not proved). The compiler takes its names from a
      counter. Generalizing FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesCallsProofs
      .rebuild_unbox_modes_with_refines_cc_never_gen (of another stream)
      to any first name, as M1 did for the local unboxing, would remove
      it.
    - The checks of the calls statement on the erased unit with
      `alias_consts T` (`check_closed` among them): that the completion
      of the least solution of the graph with regions passes
      `check_closed` on the erased graph is not proved (it needs a use of
      the constants wherever the variable of an end has one, which the
      least solution has where any constant reaches a use; `local_pair`
      has it).
  - Left.
    - The commutation of the mode of the fix with calls, code and
      wrappers (`rebuild_apply_u`, `rebuild_code_u`,
      `make_apply_wrapper_u`): the parts of a call are the same in the
      two rebuilds (the decisions read the code deps, whose nodes are not
      the constants, `kept_code_mask` and `apply_never_returns` read the
      constructors and the sources, which the erasure keeps), and the
      wrapper has parameters of the kinds of the return arity of the call
      and of the leaves of unboxed fields, none of the kind `Region` on
      `regions_plain` with no field of that kind, so it is erased to
      itself; code is erased in its body alone. Not written: it is the
      union of the cases of the commutations of rg2 and rg3 with those
      parts (an estimated 800 lines), for units whose closures are sets
      of one closure with no value slot and are not unboxed (the
      closures of `calls_unboxing`).
    - The proof that `alias_consts` of the least solution passes the
      checks on the erased graph; the names premise (above).
    - Units with a parameter of the kind `Region`, code that returns a
      region, a local set of several closures (M3, not attempted).
  - Nothing found false.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 1020 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 7
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperRegionsFixedSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperRegionsUnboxSpec.v, FlambdaReaperRegionsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperRegionsCcSpec.v, FlambdaReaperRebuildSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesSpec.v depend on no axiom outside the
    ledger (own-assum.sh: 97 statements, 97 closed under the global
    context).
- The analysis of the first rebuilt unit related to that of the unit: the
  premise `used_reach_covered` of the fragments read off the unit's own
  analysis derived, rather than checked against a second analysis, for
  the units whose first rebuild renumbers the nodes it makes (code, calls
  and primitives dropped), gives poison to nodes with no use, makes calls
  direct, changes calling conventions and adds wrappers
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin2{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin3{Spec,Proofs}.v, new). The tables of
  the first rebuilt unit are built from the unit's: renamed, and with any
  source at the nodes with no use. What the class asks of the first
  rebuild is a check of its facts against those and the tables of the
  unit; that every unit of the fragments passes it (the general case,
  M4) is not proved. Done: M1 (renaming), M2 (poison), M3 (calls made
  direct, changes of calling convention that delete trailing parameters
  and results, wrappers).
  - What was in the way.
    - The check `facts_within` of the earlier entry compares the facts of
      the two traversals node for node. The traversal numbers the nodes
      it makes in turn, so that a first rebuild that drops a call (a
      handler made `Invalid`), a primitive or code makes those of what
      follows under other numbers.
    - Poison: the rebuild gives a constant (`Node_all_constants`, which
      has any source) in place of a variable with no use
      (`rewrite_simple`) and of an argument whose parameter has no use
      (the masks of the arguments). The facts of the unit with that
      variable become facts with the constants, which no renaming gives
      back, and the rules then give any source to what reads them.
    - A call made direct gives, with `Auto`, two fresh nodes, the aliases
      on any source of the callee and the aliases of the apply dep; a
      change of calling convention makes fewer nodes for the code and
      fewer arguments for the call, and a wrapper has fresh variables as
      parameters.
  - The route.
    - The rules read the graph only in their premises and name no node
      (FlambdaReaperGraphSpec.closed). So a solution of the rules on a
      graph `G`, taken at the renamed nodes, is a solution on any graph
      that a renaming takes into `G` (`closed_renamed`: a graph
      homomorphism, every rule of the 43 at once; `local_reads_used`
      likewise). The tables at the renamed nodes are `pull_tables r T`:
      each tuple of `T` with each node replaced by each node that `r`
      takes to it (`preimage`); their solution is that of `T` at the
      renamed nodes (`pull_tables_pulls`). The reach, `over_reach` and
      `rem_reach` read the constructors of the fields that are not real
      and the inputs `any_source` by membership, so that with a renaming
      that keeps the code ids and the variables of the unit
      (`renaming_ok`) the reach of the first rebuilt unit with these
      tables is within that of the unit (`flow_reach2_ren`,
      `over_reach_ren`, `rem_reach_ren`), and `used_reach_covered` holds.
      The run of the first rebuilt unit is then as that reach says
      (`first_runs` of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithinProofs.v), and
      the checks of the fragments, monotone in the reach
      (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesUnitFlowProofs.unit_flow3_covered,
      `unit_never_covered`), give the run premises of
      `rebuild_unbox_modes_refines_cc_over3` and `_never`
      (`tables_flow3_refines_cc`, `tables_never_refines_cc`, which take
      tables of the first rebuilt unit closed in the sense of the rules
      rather than of `check_closed`).
    - Poison (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin3Proofs.v):
      `poisoned_tables F T` is `T` with any source, a source and an alias
      to the constants at every node with no use (`unused_nodes`: of the
      facts and of the aliases of `T`). A node with no use flows only to
      nodes with no use, along aliases, accessors and uses
      (`unused_alias`, `unused_accessor`, `unused_use`, from
      `has_usage_alias`, `has_usage_accessor`, `any_usage_use`), so that
      the any source added stays among them, and the reach at a variable
      with a use is that of `T` (`flow_reach2_used`, `rem_reach_used`).
      `poison_closed`: these tables are closed on `poison_graph`, the
      facts of `F`, the poison facts (a constructor or an argument of
      `F` with the constants in place of the node it holds or gives) and
      the alias, use, propagate, alias-on-any-source, `any_usage` and
      `any_source_if_used` facts that the tables satisfy; each of the 43
      rules checked, the guarded ones included (a negative guard on
      `any_source` holds of fewer nodes, so asks less). What it needs is
      `unit_poison_ok` (the constants have any usage in `T`; at the
      nodes with no use the rules on `F` ask nothing more: aliases on
      their any source hold, they are not called with arguments and are
      given only to calls with a use, what holds them in a field has a
      source, no local field is read from them) and, of each poison fact,
      that what the rules alias to it (readers of the field through the
      uses and sources of `T`, parameters of the call) has no use, that
      the block has a source and the call a use, and that a local field
      with any usage escapes with the constants
      (`poison_constructor_ok`, `poison_argument_ok`).
    - Calls made direct need no rule of their own: their new facts are
      aliases, which a renaming makes aliases of the unit's tables (the
      fresh node of the witness to the node of the call of the unit,
      which the tables alias to the witness of known arity of the code;
      that of the closure to the closure, to which they alias
      `my_closure`), and aliases on any source of a callee that has none.
      A change of calling convention gives fewer facts, under a renaming;
      the parameters of a wrapper, fresh variables (not of the unit, so
      that `renaming_ok` lets them be renamed), are renamed to those of
      the continuation of the call, which have the same accessors of the
      results of the call.
  - New statements, all proved:
    - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin2Spec.v (9): `within2_closed`
      (`pull_tables r T` is closed and `local_reads_used` on `F1` where
      `facts_within_renamed r F1 F`), `within2_used_reach_covered`
      (`used_reach_covered F T F1 (pull_tables r T) e`, with
      `renaming_ok r e`, so that the statements of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesUnitFlowSpec.v apply with these tables
      for `T1` where their checks pass); `renamed_flow3_refines_cc`,
      `renamed_flow3_observably_local`,
      `renamed_flow3_refines_observably`, `renamed_never_refines_cc`,
      `renamed_never_observably_local`,
      `renamed_never_refines_observably` (those of
      FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithinSpec.v with `renaming_ok r e` and
      `facts_within_renamed r` in place of `facts_within`; the earlier
      ones are the case of `r` empty); `renamed_example` (the unit of
      `never_ind_example` with a call in the handler 20, whose parameter
      has no source: the first rebuild makes the handler `Invalid`, and
      the nodes of the calls that follow come three numbers lower; its
      facts are not within the unit's, are up to the renaming given, and
      it refines its rebuilt unit up to observation by
      `renamed_never_refines_observably`).
    - FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin3Spec.v (11):
      `analysis_within_closed`, `analysis_within_used_reach_covered` (the
      same for `pull_tables r (poisoned_tables F T)` where
      `analysis_within is_local r F1 F T`);
      `analysis_within_flow3_refines_cc`,
      `analysis_within_flow3_observably_local`,
      `analysis_within_flow3_refines_observably`,
      `analysis_within_never_refines_cc`,
      `analysis_within_never_observably_local`,
      `analysis_within_never_refines_observably` (with `analysis_within`
      in place of `facts_within_renamed`); `poison_example` (the unit of
      `poison_reach`: no renaming keeping the constants takes its first
      rebuild's facts within the unit's, as the unit gives no constant as
      an argument; it passes `analysis_within` with no renaming, and
      refines its rebuilt unit up to observation);
      `direct_example` (a unit that applies the closure of the code 50 at
      known arity, 50 not using its parameter: the first rebuild makes
      the call direct and deletes the parameter; no renaming takes its
      facts within the unit's, which have no alias on any source; it
      passes `analysis_within` with the renaming given, and refines its
      rebuilt unit up to observation); `wrapper_example` (the unit of
      `flow_example`: the first rebuild gives poison to the results of 50
      and 51, makes the call of 52 return to a wrapper, and changes the
      calling convention of 52; it is not within the unit's facts under
      the renaming given, passes `analysis_within` under it, the
      parameter `x63` of the wrapper renamed to `x30`, and refines its
      rebuilt unit up to observation).
    - Checked by computation (scratch, not stated): the constants have
      any usage in the least solutions of the units of `poison_reach`,
      `flow_example`, `flow2_example`, `flow3_example` and
      `never_ind_example`.
  - Premises added, and why: none to existing statements. The new
    statements have those of the statements of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithinSpec.v, with the check
    (`facts_within_renamed` or `analysis_within`) of the first rebuild
    against the unit and a renaming with `renaming_ok`, in place of
    `facts_within`. `analysis_within` asks that the constants have any
    usage in the unit's tables: the tables built alias every node with
    no use to the constants, so that without it `usages_alias` would ask
    the constants to have the usages of such nodes, which the unit's
    tables need not give them (and `any_usage_argument`,
    `any_usage_constructor` would ask any usage of the constants given
    as poison). It held of every unit tried. The new statements need no
    analysis of the first rebuilt unit: the check compares its facts
    with the unit's facts and tables.
  - Left:
    - `used_reach_covered` in general (M4): that every unit of the
      fragments passes `analysis_within` with some renaming. It needs a
      relation between the two runs of the traversal (the unit's and its
      first rebuild's), with the accumulator (the counter, the code deps,
      the apply and closure deps, the symbols) and the environment, of
      the size of FlambdaReaperUnusedCodeTraversalProofs.v and more, and
      the solution's side of it (that the poison facts and the facts of
      the calls made direct meet the conditions of the check), which the
      least solutions give but which is a property of the least solution
      of the unit (a field poisoned because it has no use has readers
      with no use through the uses of `T`, while the rules alias to it
      the readers through the uses and the sources).
    - Units out of the class: a parameter deleted before a kept one,
      which renumbers the parameter and argument facts that follow (the
      number is not a node); closures of deleted code, whose witness the
      traversal makes fresh with any source and two constructors that no
      renaming takes to the unit's.
    - The least solution of the first rebuilt unit (the `T1` of the
      examples of FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesUnitFlowSpec.v) compared
      with the tables built here: the negative guards still stand in the
      way, but only through any source, which the tables built here give
      at the nodes with no use.
  - Nothing found false.
  - Existing statements and files unchanged; rocq/Makefile lists the four
    new files (after the Within files). `make -k all` (with `claims`:
    1040 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 20 new) passes; the statements of
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin2Spec.v and
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin3Spec.v depend on no axiom outside
    the ledger (`own-assum.sh`: 20 statements, 20 closed under the global
    context).
  - The examples are proved on the terms the statements name: the facts
    and code deps of a computed unit are rewritten into the form of the
    statements by equalities proved by `reflexivity`, and the lists of
    facts of the first rebuilt unit are reached through lemmas on any
    facts (`facts_within_renamed_lists`, `in_rename_argument`); applying
    the statements to the computed terms directly made the kernel
    evaluate the traversal at `Qed` (minutes for each).
- The two table checks of the changes of representation of sets of
  closures that were still checks (FlambdaReaperTraversalSlots{Spec,Proofs}.v,
  FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesPlain{Spec,Proofs}.v and
  FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesAlike{Spec,Proofs}.v, new). The first, that
  the simple of a field of a representation that is not unboxed is not
  unboxed (`plain_simple` in `plain_c`), is derived for every `is_local`,
  under the premises of `changed_field_used` and with no new premise, from
  a new invariant of the traversal; a fourth fragment drops it. The
  second, "unboxed alike" (`unboxed_field_pairs` of a field unboxed and
  the variable it holds), does not follow: a unit of the premises, on
  which the compiler's recursions end with none of their fatal errors,
  has a slot and its variable unboxed to different depths. The existing
  statements are unchanged in name, type and meaning.
  - What was in the way.
    - `plain_simple`: the inversion of `alias y _` in a least solution for
      a read `y` of the slot, which needs that nothing but its read puts
      anything into the variable of a projection of a value slot. No
      invariant of the traversal said so:
      `TypesTraversalUnaliasedProofs.named_tgt` asks nothing of such a
      variable, and `LeastProofs.BoundOnce.vinv` is of units with no
      closure. The inversions of FlambdaReaperLeastProofs.v
      (`alias_into`, ...) are of a field that is not local, and a value
      slot may be.
    - "Unboxed alike": the fields of the variable are read from all its
      uses and from those that `add_usages_through_function_slots` adds,
      which the reads of the slot need not see.
  - The route.
    - The invariant (FlambdaReaperTraversalSlotsProofs.v, `inv`): each
      fact puts something (`AutoWidgets.into_nodes`: the target of an
      alias, a read, a use, a propagation, a parameter, the base of a
      constructor or a parameter, any source) into the variable of a
      projection of a value slot only if it is its read, and reads a
      value slot only as such a projection; the nodes of the deps that
      facts are later put into (the nodes of the code deps, the call
      witnesses of the apply deps, the closures of the closure deps) and
      the parameters of the continuations in scope are not such
      variables (`free`). Each step keeps it, for any flag, where the
      unit binds each variable once: the other steps put things into
      parameters, the variables of other `Let`s, symbols and made nodes.
      `IndirectDeadFramesTraversalProofs.Chains.only_read_into_of` turns
      it into `only_read_into`.
    - `plain_simple` (FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesPlainProofs.v). Say the
      simple `n` that the closures of the set hold at the slot `w` (they
      hold it alone: `closure_value_slots`, `node_exact`) is dominated by
      an allocation `a0` to unbox. The field's step in
      `mk_unboxed_fields` (`mk_step_in`, `URP.field_step`) leaves it a
      variable only if: it is `Use_unknown`, and then `n` has any usage
      (`field_used_top_any_usage`), so has `a0`
      (`LeastProofs.sources_any_usage`), which is then in
      `cannot_unbox`; or its source is not `One_source a0`, which it is
      (`field_source_a0`: `n` has no any source and `a0` is its one
      source with a source); or a read `y` of the field is not to be
      unboxed (`case_read`). `y` is the variable of a projection of `w`
      off a node `u` (`slot_reads_projected`), only read into
      (`slot_read_only`), and an alias of `n` (`field_used_as_alias`).
      Of the three ways it is of `field_used_as` of a closure `c` of the
      set (`field_used_as_cases`): off a use `u` of `c`, where
      `crr0_any_source_usage` and `crr0_other_source` give `u` no any
      source and `c` its one source with a source; read with any source
      from a local field while `c` escapes, which `crr0_local_reading`
      excludes; or off a node `u` of which `c` is a source (a local
      field), where the guard of `sources` gives `u` no any source and
      `crr0_local_sources` makes `c` its one source with a source. No
      closure of the set is in `cannot_change_representation0`
      (`closure_not_crr0`: it holds the allocation of `tcr` at a function
      slot, `crr1_function_slot`). With the local inversions
      (`alias_into_l`, `any_source_into_l`, `sources_into_l`, which
      include the local rules), what `y` is an alias of is `n` or has no
      source (`read_aliases`), so `y` has no any source and `a0` as its
      one source with a source (`read_dominated`): it is to be unboxed.
    - The fragment: `repr_field_frag4_ok` asks of the simple of a field
      not unboxed only `not_changed` (the other conjunct of `plain_c`,
      false of the construction: `changed_in_slot_counterexample`);
      `repr_unboxing_frag4_sound` gives `repr_unboxing_frag3_nr` through
      `ClosuresTablesSlotsProofs.with_mono`.
    - "Unboxed alike": the unit `SlotOfBlock` of
      FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesAlikeSpec.v, computed.
  - New statements, all proved.
    - FlambdaReaperTraversalSlotsSpec.v: `slot_read_only_statement` (in
      the facts of the traversal, any flag, of a unit that binds each
      variable once, the variable of a projection of a value slot is
      `only_read_into` its read, with no alias that holds where the
      closure has any source), `slot_reads_projected_statement` (each
      read of a value slot is that of such a projection, of its slot,
      off the node of its simple, into its variable). `slot_projection`
      is new there.
    - FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesPlainSpec.v:
      `changed_field_plain_statement` (with the premises of
      `changed_field_used`, the simple that `vs` has at the slot of a
      field not unboxed is `plain_simple`);
      `repr_unboxing_frag4_sound_statement` (`repr_unboxing_frag4_nr`
      implies `repr_unboxing_frag3_nr`);
      `rebuild_repr_frag4_refines_cc_statement`,
      `rebuild_repr_frag4_observably_statement`,
      `rebuild_repr_frag4_local_statement` (the `frag3` ones with the
      weaker fragment). `repr_field_frag4_ok`, `changed_set_frag4_ok` and
      `repr_unboxing_frag4_nr` are new there.
    - FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesAlikeSpec.v:
      `unboxing_checked_ends_statement` (`unboxing_checked n block
      fields`, a check that the compiler's recursion from `block` and
      `fields` makes no chain of more than `n` nested calls and stops on
      none of two fatal errors, a field read into names some to be
      unboxed and some not or all to be unboxed with several sources,
      gives `UnboxingFuelSpec.unboxing_ends`), and
      `unboxed_alike_counterexample_statement` (below).
  - Premises added, and why: none. `changed_field_plain` has the premises
    of `changed_field_used` and holds for every `is_local`; the
    traversal statements need only that each variable is bound once
    (the compiler opens every binder at fresh names). `unboxing_checked`
    is not a premise of any statement: it shows that the counterexample
    meets the compiler's conditions.
  - Left.
    - "Unboxed alike" is still a check (`repr_field_frag_ok`, through
      `repr_field_frag3_ok`, for a field unboxed), as is `not_changed` of
      the simple of a field not unboxed. A derivation would need, besides
      the recursions of the set and of the variable ending with no fatal
      error (`unboxing_checked` gives the first): that every node read
      off a use at a function slot be a closure of a set that holds the
      slot (a typing of projections of function slots, which the
      compiler's units meet and no premise states); with it, that each
      use of the variable's unboxing is one of its allocation, of a
      closure of its set, or of the `my_closure` of code it is called
      with, whose reads are aliases of what the allocation holds (the
      closure deps alias `my_closure` to the closures of the code where
      the code has any usage, which a call of it seems to give; not
      proved), so that a read of any usage there gives the source of the
      field any usage; the monotonicity of
      `get_direct_usages`, `add_usages_through_function_slots` and
      `get_fields` in the uses; the fuel (the slot one level below the
      top: the chain bound of the set's recursion); and that no symbol is
      dominated by an allocation to unbox, for the simple to be a
      variable (an invariant of the facts into symbols: `cu0_symbol`
      then applies).
  - Found false: "unboxed alike"
    (`unboxed_alike_counterexample_statement`). The unit `SlotOfBlock`
    makes `x11 = [7]`, `x10 = [x11]` and the closure `x61` of code 51
    with `x10` in its slot 80, and calls `x61`; code 51 reads the slot
    into `x44`, field 0 of it into `x45` and field 0 of that, which it
    switches on; a handler that is never applied projects the function
    slot 72 off `x10`, a block, into `x90`, reads field 0 of `x90` into
    `x91` and returns it. The unit is in the flow fragment, well formed,
    binds each variable once, its least solution passes
    `analysis_tables` with no code that cannot change its calling
    convention; `x11` and `x10` are to be unboxed and `x61` changes its
    representation; `unboxing_checked` holds (bound 2) of the recursion
    of the representation of `x61` and of that of the unboxing of `x10`.
    The slot 80 is unboxed two levels deep (field 0 of field 0), as the
    reads of the slot are (`x44`, unboxed alike), but the field 0 of
    `x10` is a variable: the uses of `x10` include those of `x90`
    (`add_usages_through_function_slots` follows any read at a function
    slot), from which field 0 is read into `x91`, of any usage, so the
    field is `Use_unknown`; `x90` has no source, so that the any usage
    of `x91` reaches no allocation and `x11` is still to be unboxed.
    `unboxed_field_pairs` of the two is `None`, and the slot fails
    `repr_field_frag_ok`. The
    compiler, on the same unit, would stop with a fatal error in the
    rebuild of the set of `x61` (`fold2_subset_u` of rebuild.ml,
    "[fold2_unboxed_subset]"); it never projects a function slot off a
    block. The same place of rebuild.ml stops on a slot not unboxed that
    holds an unboxed name: `changed_field_plain` shows that it is never
    reached on the fragment.
  - Files: new FlambdaReaperTraversalSlots{Spec,Proofs}.v,
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesPlain{Spec,Proofs}.v,
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesAlike{Spec,Proofs}.v (listed in
    rocq/Makefile beside the related entries); the comments of
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesSlotsSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesFieldsSpec.v that described the two
    checks as left now point to the new files. No statement, proof or
    model file changed otherwise.
  - `make -k all` (with `claims`: 1049 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 9
    new) passes; the statements of FlambdaReaperTraversalSlotsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesPlainSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesAlikeSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesSlotsSpec.v and
    FlambdaReaperClosuresTablesFieldsSpec.v depend on no axiom outside
    the ledger (own-assum.sh: 24 statements, 24 closed under the global
    context).
- The traversal of a unit related to that of its first rebuild, step by
  step, for the plain rebuild (dropped code, [Let]s, handlers, calls and
  primitives, and poison; no change of calling convention, no call made
  direct that the traversal reads otherwise, no wrapper)
  (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin4{Spec,SimProofs,Proofs}.v, new).
  The facts part of [analysis_within] is split from the solution's side
  ([facts_within_poison], [poison_sites_met]), the class of units is
  stated ([plain_unit], [plain_rebuild]), and every step of the two runs
  for the constructs of the class but the [Let_static] of the code is
  proved to keep a relation between the accumulators up to a renaming of
  the made nodes. The induction over the terms that composes the steps,
  the code deps and the delayed deps are not done, so the statement that
  every unit of the class passes the facts part (M1) is not proved; M2-M4
  not started. Found: not every unit of the fragments passes
  [analysis_within] ([box_poison_statement]).
  - What was in the way.
    - The two runs number the nodes they make in turn: where the rebuild
      drops a term, the run of the unit makes nodes that the other does
      not, and the nodes of what follows differ. A relation between the
      runs must carry the renaming as it grows, and a node of the first
      rebuilt run already in a fact must keep its image when the renaming
      grows: every made node of the accumulator is below its counter.
    - The rebuild also drops parameters of handlers (masks), rewrites
      simples into poison, filters value slots and the arms of a
      [Switch], and the traversal of the first rebuilt unit compacts the
      arms again: the relation must follow the masks of the rebuild
      through the environment of continuations, and keep the order of
      what makes nodes (the arms, the closure deps).
    - Poison that no check accepts: a boxed number with a use and no use
      of its field becomes [Simple (Const Poison)] in the first rebuild,
      an alias of a node with a use to the constants.
  - The route (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin4SimProofs.v, 2840
    lines, no statement).
    - [sim r a1 a]: each fact of the accumulator [a1] of the first
      rebuilt run, renamed by [r], is a fact of [a] (the unit's run) or
      one with the constants in place of a node of a fact of [a] where the
      rebuild gives poison ([pois]: the from-node of an alias, a use, an
      [any_usage] with no use; a field of a block from a node or of a
      field with no use; an argument); the apply deps renamed are those
      of [a], the closure deps renamed a sublist of those of [a]
      ([subrel]), with code ids that [a1] does not prepare not prepared in
      [a] ([code_ok]); the symbols of [a1] are those of [a]; every made
      node of [a1] is below its counter ([below]); the keys of [r] are
      made nodes. [stepped r a1 r' a1' a']: [sim r' a1' a'] and [r'] renames
      as [r] what is below the counter of [a1] ([ext]).
    - Generic steps: both runs make a node ([sim_fresh], [step_fresh],
      [step_fresh_list]: the renaming takes the one to the other), add
      facts ([sim_add]), record symbols, apply deps and closure deps; the
      run of the unit alone ([grows], [sim_grow]).
    - The environment ([env_rel], [ps_rel], [conts_rel]): the parameters
      of a continuation bound by a handler are its variables in the
      unit's run and those that the mask of the rebuild keeps in the
      other; those of the return and exception continuations of code and
      of the unit are made nodes that the renaming takes to the unit's.
    - The constructs, the rebuilt one against that of the unit:
      [step_find_cont], [step_cond_any_usage], [step_cond_alias], the call
      widgets of known and unknown arity ([step_known_widget],
      [step_unknown_deps], [step_unknown_widget]: the groups of the
      arguments have the same shapes, [forall2_groups]), [step_add_apply],
      [step_call_kind] (for call kinds related by [ck_rel]: the same, of
      known arity for known arity or direct, or a direct call of code of
      the unit read as of known arity where direct calls are not
      preserved), [step_apply] (a call with no wrapper and whose
      exception continuation keeps its first parameter: the return
      parameters are those of the unit, the extra arguments of the
      exception filtered by the mask after the first, [step_aliases]),
      [step_apply_cont], [step_switch] (the arms kept are a sublist of the
      compacted arms, whose keys are distinct, so that compacting them
      again changes nothing: [keyed_compact_nodup]; [step_fold_subrel]),
      [step_exn_handler] (the first parameter of an exception handler is
      kept), the [Let]s ([step_named]: a [Simple], a primitive
      ([step_prim_map], [step_prim_default], [step_block_deps]: a field of
      a block given poison where it or its node has no use), a
      projection; an accessor of a variable with a use reads a node with
      a use in closed tables, so the rebuild does not rewrite it;
      [step_closure], a set of one function, its value slots filtered and
      rewritten).
    - [grows_traverse]: the run of the unit alone through a term of the
      class keeps [sim], for what the rebuild drops or makes [Invalid].
  - New statements, all proved
    (FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin4Spec.v, 2):
    - `within_poison_analysis_within`: [unit_poison_ok],
      [facts_within_poison F T (rename_facts r F1)] and
      [poison_sites_met is_local F T (rename_facts r F1)] give
      [analysis_within is_local r F1 F T]: the check is its facts part
      and the solution's side of the fields and arguments given poison.
    - `box_poison` (the counterexample, below).
  - Premises added, and why: none to existing statements. The class
    (defined, for the statement left open): [plain_unit] (code first, of
    distinct ids, then a term that binds no static; sets of one function;
    no [Local_allocation]; no [Set_of_closures] or [Project_value_slot];
    a [Let] of a kind other than [Value] and the naked numbers binds a
    primitive of regions, as the rebuild keeps such a [Let] with no use
    and would give its accessor poison) and [plain_rebuild] (no wrapper:
    the continuation of a call keeps all its parameters, its exception
    continuation its first; [call_kind_kept]; a set of closures with a
    use has code with a use or code the unit does not bind, as the
    closures of deleted code get a fresh witness no renaming takes back;
    a boxed number with a use has a use of its field, by
    [box_poison_statement]); and every code of the unit in
    [cannot_change], so that the first rebuild is the rebuild of
    FlambdaReaperRebuild.v.
  - Left:
    - M1: the induction over the terms of the class that composes the
      steps (a [Let_cont] that the rebuild drops needs the relation of the
      environments only at the continuations free in the rebuilt term,
      [free_continuations_of_expr]; [env_rel] now asks it at all), the
      [Let_static] of the code (the code deps of [prepare_code] related,
      code deleted made by the unit's run alone, [traverse_code]) and the
      delayed deps ([resolve_delayed_deps], the closure deps in order),
      then the statement: for every unit of the class with closed
      tables, a renaming with [renaming_ok] such that
      [facts_within_poison F T (rename_facts r F1)].
    - M2: [poison_sites_met] and [unit_poison_ok] from the least solution
      (for an argument given poison the reason, a parameter with no use
      among the witnesses of the callee, is to be recorded in [pois]).
    - M3, M4: calls made direct with [Auto] (two fresh nodes),
      wrappers, changes of calling convention, deletions before a kept
      parameter, closures of deleted code. Wrappers share their
      parameter names ([names_from] of [first_fresh]) across calls, so
      that two wrappers for continuations with other parameters cannot be
      renamed to both: a premise or another check is needed there (not
      stated as a theorem).
  - Found false: that every unit of the fragments passes
    [analysis_within] with some renaming. `box_poison_statement`: the
    unit [let x10 = Box_number 0. in let x11 = Is_int x10 in return x11]
    (flag [Never], least tables) satisfies [plain_unit], [check_closed],
    and [calls_unboxing_unit_flow3_nr] (the fragment read off its own
    analysis), [plain_rebuild] fails (at the box only), and for every
    renaming with [renaming_ok], [analysis_within] is [false]: the first
    rebuild binds [x10] to poison (its field of boxed number has no use,
    [Is_int] reads another field), its traversal gives the alias
    [(x10, constants)], which the unit's facts do not have and the
    unit's tables with poison do not give ([x10] has a use), and the
    renaming keeps [x10] and the constants. The unit reads [Is_int] of a
    boxed float, so that it is not of OCaml's types; the first rebuild
    is [Let x10 = Poison in Invalid].
  - rocq/Makefile lists the three new files (after the Within3 files).
    `make -k all` (with `claims`: 1051 lines `proves FlambdaReaper`, 2
    new) passes; `own-assum.sh` on
    FlambdaReaperUnboxingRefinesWithin4Spec: 2 statements, 2 closed under
    the global context. Existing statements and files unchanged.
- The heap in the statements of the results of code, of the parameters of
  code and of the fragment `loose`
  (FlambdaReaperTypes{ReturnsHeap,ParamsHeap,LooseHeap}{Spec,Proofs}.v,
  new; FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsProofs.v and
  FlambdaReaperTypesCallsHeapProofs.v changed). The statements hold; the
  existing statements are unchanged in name, type and meaning. Of the
  milestones of the round, the first (the heap in those statements) is
  done; the results of code for the unit rebuilt with unboxing across
  calls and the parameters that it unboxes at the nodes of
  `code_dep.params` are left (no budget).
  - What was in the way. The statements of FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsSpec.v,
    FlambdaReaperTypesParamsSpec.v (and ParamsTraversalSpec.v) and
    FlambdaReaperTypesLooseSpec.v, with their `Used` forms, were of
    `in_flow_fragment`: the alias of the node of a result to the argument
    of a return (FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsProofs.return_alias) was read off
    the edges of the traversal of the flow fragment, the rebuilt values
    were related by `rebuilt_value` (no location), and the static sets kept
    whole (`static_sets_whole`, asked by the fragment `loose`) were proved
    of the simulations without the heap only
    (FlambdaReaperTypesCallsProofs.cc_states_used).
  - The route.
    - The alias at a return, with the heap
      (FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsProofs.v): `return_alias_gen` takes the
      fragment `RP.in_fragment hb` (the edges of the traversal with the
      primitives of the heap if `hb`, which only the edges of primitives
      read); `return_alias` keeps its name and type and is its instance at
      `false`.
    - The values rebuilt with the heap at the nodes
      (FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsHeapProofs.v): `rebuilt_value_heap_alias`
      (what `rebuilt_value_heap` relates at a node it relates at every alias
      of it, a location being related by the correspondence alone),
      `return_rebuilt_heap` (the argument of a return has in the rebuilt run
      a value rebuilt with the heap under the metadata of the node of the
      result), `rewrite_result_type_at_sound_heap`
      (FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsProofs.rewrite_result_type_at_sound_in of
      the values with each location erased: the types never read the heap,
      FlambdaReaperTypesHeapProofs.type_satisfied_erase).
      FlambdaReaperTypesParamsHeapProofs.param_node_facts_heap: the check
      `params_aliased` of the traversal of a unit of `in_heap_fragment`
      (FlambdaReaperTypesParamsTraversalSpec.params_aliased_traversal)
      gives, as FlambdaReaperTypesParamsProofs.param_node_facts, that a
      value rebuilt with the heap at the node of a parameter is rebuilt

    - The static sets whole, with the heap
      (FlambdaReaperTypesCallsHeapProofs.v): `statics_rel_whole_heap`

    - The states are those of `cc_states_heap` (the rebuild with changes of

  - New statements, all proved:
    - FlambdaReaperTypesReturnsHeapSpec.v (8):
      `code_result_type_end_to_end_heap` (plain rebuild) and

  - Premises added, and why: none. The statements take `heap_premises`
    (`used_premises` with `in_heap_fragment` in place of
    `in_flow_fragment`, so their `Used` forms are covered) and the premises
    of the statements they extend; `static_sets_whole` follows from
